# MyLite ↔ MySQL 8.4.9 compatibility

This document is the initial compatibility target inventory for MyLite against MySQL 8 LTS, , , , , , currently MySQL 8.4.9. It replaces the seed matrix with a MyLite-specific catalog of SQL grammar, runtime semantics, metadata, protocol, and operational surfaces that must be tracked as implementation progresses.

## Legend

| Mark | Meaning |
| :-: | --- |
| ✅ | Supported with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible behavior and runtime comparison tests for relevant results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, side effects, and protocol details. |
| 🟡 | Implemented with documented compatibility gaps, reduced fidelity, or MyLite-specific behavior that is covered by tests. |
| ⚪ | Accepted at parse or API level and intentionally handled as an embedded-compatible no-op, placeholder, warning, or diagnostic. |
| ❌ | Not implemented, rejected, or not yet MySQL-runtime verified in MyLite. |

## Prioritization rubric

Priority is a delivery-order signal, not a relaxation of correctness:

- `top`: Blocks WordPress Core installation, ordinary page requests, core schema upgrades, common `wpdb` flows, MySQL client interoperability, or the highest-install WordPress.org plugins.
- `high`: Common in popular plugins, ecommerce/forms/security/migration tooling, admin diagnostics, schema migration paths, or mainstream MySQL application code.
- `medium`: Useful compatibility for less common plugin features, advanced SQL, metadata inspection, or broader ecosystem coverage that does not usually block WordPress-first adoption.
- `low`: Exotic, server-administration-only, storage-engine-specific, replication/cluster-only, deprecated, debug-only, or rarely used compatibility surface.

## Maintenance rules

- Keep this file as an implementation contract, not a marketing scorecard.
- Split rows when syntax, behavior, metadata, warnings, or side effects need independent tests.
- Document deliberate embedded-design incompatibilities explicitly instead of silently dropping them.
- Keep `COMPATIBILITY.md`, feature guides, design notes, and MySQL-runtime tests in sync.
- Prefer MySQL 8.4.9 behavior over convenience, SQLite defaults, or older MySQL/MariaDB behavior.

## 1. SQL statement surface

The parser should eventually recognize the full MySQL grammar. Unsupported embedded-server features may still be accepted with a MySQL-compatible diagnostic, warning, or placeholder when that is safer for applications than a syntax error.

### 1.1 Data definition statements

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ALTER DATABASE` / `ALTER SCHEMA` | 🟡 | high | Database default character set, collation, encryption, and read-only options. | Implemented for the MyLite schema catalog, including omitted-name default-schema targeting and validation for the initial `utf8mb4`, `utf8mb3` / `utf8`, `latin1`, and `binary` charset/collation registry, with `utf8` normalized to `utf8mb3` and warning 3719; full read-only enforcement, privileges, and broader warning records are deferred. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md) and [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md). |
| `ALTER EVENT` | ❌ | medium | Event scheduler metadata and body changes. |  |
| `ALTER FUNCTION` | ❌ | medium | Stored-function metadata characteristics. |  |
| `ALTER INSTANCE` | ❌ | low | Instance reload, TLS, keyring, and master-key operations with embedded-compatible behavior. |  |
| `ALTER LOGFILE GROUP` | ❌ | low | NDB logfile group syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `ALTER PROCEDURE` | ❌ | medium | Stored-procedure metadata characteristics. |  |
| `ALTER SERVER` | ❌ | low | Foreign server metadata changes. |  |
| `ALTER TABLE` | 🟡 | top | Full table rebuild/in-place/instant surface; see section 3.2. | Column operations are implemented for existing MyLite base tables: `ADD`, `DROP`, `RENAME COLUMN`, `CHANGE`, `MODIFY`, and `ALTER COLUMN SET/DROP DEFAULT`, including multi-action atomic shadow rewrites, `FIRST`/`AFTER`, default backfill and mutation, column/index catalog rewrites, duplicate-index warnings, foreign-key column dependency rejection/metadata rewrites, and non-default `ALGORITHM`/`LOCK` rejection before mutation. Key operations are implemented for metadata-backed `ADD PRIMARY KEY`, `ADD UNIQUE`, `ADD INDEX` / `ADD KEY`, `ADD FULLTEXT INDEX`, `DROP PRIMARY KEY`, `DROP INDEX` / `DROP KEY`, `RENAME INDEX` / `RENAME KEY`, and `ALTER INDEX` visibility, including existing-row primary/unique validation, generated index names, warnings, full-text parser/comment metadata, duplicate-key write-path effects, mixed column/key atomic rewrites, and foreign-key dependency rejection for dependent index drops. `ADD/DROP/ALTER CHECK` is implemented for supported persistent and temporary base tables, including generated names, DDL-time expression validation for ADD CHECK, existing-row validation, `NOT ENFORCED`, enforcement toggling, metadata cleanup, MySQL-compatible duplicate/missing/violation diagnostics, and statement-atomic execution when mixed with supported column, index, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` table-option actions. FK-only `ADD/DROP FOREIGN KEY` is implemented for supported persistent base tables. Table options now include `AUTO_INCREMENT [=] integer` for supported base tables, including MySQL-observed generated-value floor behavior and no-op acceptance on tables without an auto-increment column while preserving any create-time table-option value. Table rename is implemented for supported base tables through `ALTER TABLE ... RENAME`, `RENAME TO`, and `RENAME AS`, including selected-schema/schema-qualified resolution, cross-schema moves, physical table renames, table/column/index/foreign-key catalog rewrites, and deterministic unsupported diagnostics for mixed rename actions. Supported `ALTER TABLE` paths now apply MySQL-compatible implicit commit before validation/execution, including failing validation and read-only transaction cases. `SPATIAL` actions mixed with other ALTER mutations parse but return deterministic unsupported diagnostics before mutation. Partition definition and maintenance operations parse as no-op parser placeholders with warning `1235`. Generated default expressions, generated-column dependencies, temporary-table rename, physical optimizer indexes, and remaining ALTER surfaces are deferred. See [ALTER TABLE column operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-column-operations/specs.md), [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), [ALTER TABLE AUTO_INCREMENT spec](docs/specs/alter-table-auto-increment/specs.md), [RENAME TABLE spec](docs/specs/rename-table/specs.md), and [table partitioning parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/table-partitioning-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `ALTER TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | General tablespace alterations and diagnostics. |  |
| `ALTER UNDO TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Undo tablespace syntax from the MySQL parser. |  |
| `ALTER VIEW` | ❌ | high | View replacement while preserving MySQL metadata and security semantics. |  |
| `CREATE DATABASE` / `CREATE SCHEMA` | 🟡 | high | Database creation syntax, defaults, warnings, and single-file mapping. | Implemented as catalog rows inside the single `.mylite` file, with `IF NOT EXISTS`, normalized charset/collation defaults for the initial registry including `utf8` to `utf8mb3` alias warning 3719, and encryption value validation; broader warning records and full charset/collation catalog coverage are deferred. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md) and [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md). |
| `CREATE EVENT` | ⚪ | medium | Scheduled event definition, body, definer, comments, and scheduler metadata. | Parser placeholder accepts representative MySQL 8.4 event syntax and executes as a no-op warning; scheduler metadata and body execution are deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `CREATE FUNCTION` (stored) | ⚪ | medium | Stored-function definition, determinism, SQL data access, security, and body semantics. | Parser placeholder accepts representative stored-function syntax and executes as a no-op warning; metadata and execution semantics are deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `CREATE FUNCTION` (loadable) | ⚪ | low | Loadable-function registration syntax with embedded-compatible diagnostics. | Parser placeholder accepts the loadable-function `SONAME` form and executes as a no-op warning; registration is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `CREATE INDEX` | 🟡 | top | Standalone index creation over MySQL index types and attributes. | Implemented for metadata-backed ordinary, unique, and standalone full-text indexes over supported persistent and temporary base tables, including schema resolution, duplicate-name and missing-column diagnostics, existing-row unique validation, prefix/order metadata for BTREE-compatible indexes, full-text character/text column validation, full-text parser/comment metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS` rows for persistent tables, temporary `SHOW INDEX` / `SHOW CREATE TABLE` metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` rows for persistent unique indexes, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` rows for persistent unique index parts, write-path conflict effects for ordinary/unique indexes through `INSERT`, ODKU, `REPLACE`, and `UPDATE`, warning 124 for the first full-text index, warning 3502 for `USING HASH`, warning 1831 for redundant BTREE indexes, and MySQL-compatible implicit commit before validation/execution including failing validation and read-only transaction paths. Full-text search, spatial, functional, multi-valued, optimizer, physical-index, and full storage-engine validation surfaces remain deferred. See [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `CREATE LOGFILE GROUP` | ❌ | low | NDB logfile group syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `CREATE PROCEDURE` | ⚪ | medium | Stored procedure parameters, body, characteristics, and diagnostics. | Parser placeholder accepts representative MySQL 8.4 procedure syntax and executes as a no-op warning; metadata, invocation, and body execution are deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `CREATE SERVER` | ❌ | low | Foreign server metadata syntax. |  |
| `CREATE SPATIAL REFERENCE SYSTEM` | ❌ | medium | Spatial reference system catalog DDL. |  |
| `CREATE TABLE` | 🟡 | top | Column definitions, constraints, indexes, table options, generated columns, and partitions; see section 3.1. | Base execution is implemented for the supported table-definition subset: schema-qualified/default-schema creation, `IF NOT EXISTS` deterministic no-op after schema-level table-option validation, SQLite physical table creation, internal table/column/index/check catalog rows, common table options, primary/unique/secondary index metadata, critical duplicate validation with MySQL-specific condition codes for duplicate table, column, key, primary-key, and CHECK constraint-name failures, and the first deterministic row-local CHECK expression validation slice, MySQL-compatible implicit commit before non-temporary `CREATE TABLE` inside an explicit transaction, `CREATE TABLE ... LIKE` metadata cloning for supported base-table catalog rows, and first-slice `CREATE TABLE ... SELECT` without predeclared definitions. Partitioned `CREATE TABLE ... PARTITION BY ...` parses as a no-op parser placeholder with warning `1235` and does not create an unpartitioned table. `TEMPORARY` is implemented for the supported table-definition subset with session-scoped metadata and shadowing. Physical write-time defaults, generated columns, inline references, broad CTAS definition merging, temporary-table transaction exceptions, executable partitioned storage, broader MySQL CHECK expression semantics, and remaining constraint/index surfaces are deferred. Foreign-key support is now split into staged catalog, metadata, DDL, enforcement, and dependency tasks. See [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md), [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE LIKE spec](docs/specs/create-table-like/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE ... SELECT spec](docs/specs/create-table-select/specs.md), [table partitioning parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/table-partitioning-parser-placeholders/specs.md), and [foreign-key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md). |
| `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE` | 🟡 | high | Session-scoped table lifecycle and name shadowing. | Implemented for the supported `CREATE TABLE` subset, including connection-local metadata, SQLite TEMP physical tables, shadowing of persistent tables, `IF NOT EXISTS` checks in the temporary namespace after schema-level table-option validation, `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE ... LIKE` cloning, first-slice `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE ... SELECT`, DML/SELECT resolution, standalone `CREATE INDEX` / `DROP INDEX`, `SHOW COLUMNS`, `DESCRIBE`, `SHOW INDEX`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, omission from `SHOW TABLES`/`INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES`, and automatic removal on connection close. Broad temporary `ALTER TABLE` surfaces and temporary-table transaction edge cases are deferred. See [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE LIKE spec](docs/specs/create-table-like/specs.md), and [CREATE TABLE ... SELECT spec](docs/specs/create-table-select/specs.md). |
| `CREATE TABLE ... LIKE` | 🟡 | high | Exact metadata cloning rules. | Implemented for supported persistent and temporary base tables, including schema-qualified/default-schema source and target resolution, empty physical table creation, source-before-target validation, same-schema alias error 1066, `IF NOT EXISTS` note 1050 after source validation, table/column/index/check metadata cloning, regenerated check names, foreign-key metadata omission, temporary source and destination handling, and non-temporary implicit commit behavior. Views, lock-table interactions, SQL-mode revalidation beyond the supported create-table surface, data/index directory options, engine attributes, and broader unsupported table-definition features remain deferred. See [CREATE TABLE LIKE spec](docs/specs/create-table-like/specs.md). |
| `CREATE TABLE ... SELECT` | 🟡 | high | CTAS type inference, default handling, indexes, and atomicity. | Implemented for `CREATE [TEMPORARY] TABLE [IF NOT EXISTS] target [table_options] [AS] SELECT ...` without predeclared columns or indexes, including SELECT-first validation, direct source-column metadata preservation without indexes or `AUTO_INCREMENT`, first-slice expression type inference, duplicate-output-column error 1060, row materialization, affected-row counts, temporary destination metadata, `IF NOT EXISTS` note 1050 after SELECT validation, and non-temporary implicit commit behavior. Explicit definition/index merging, `IGNORE`/`REPLACE`, `TABLE`/`VALUES` sources, view-specific behavior, locking clauses, and full expression inference remain deferred. See [CREATE TABLE ... SELECT spec](docs/specs/create-table-select/specs.md). |
| `CREATE TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | General and NDB tablespace syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `CREATE UNDO TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Undo tablespace syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `CREATE TRIGGER` | ⚪ | high | Trigger timing, event, ordering, body, definer, and metadata. | Parser placeholder accepts representative trigger syntax and executes as a no-op warning; metadata and trigger firing are deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `CREATE VIEW` | ❌ | high | View column names, algorithms, security, check options, and metadata. |  |
| `DROP DATABASE` / `DROP SCHEMA` | 🟡 | high | Schema removal, metadata cleanup, warnings, and embedded single-file constraints. | Implemented for schema catalog rows, including `IF EXISTS` and clearing the selected default schema; table cleanup, privilege cleanup, and warning records wait for later metadata work. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md). |
| `DROP EVENT` | ⚪ | medium | Event metadata deletion. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 drop syntax and executes as a no-op warning; metadata deletion is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DROP FUNCTION` (stored) | ⚪ | medium | Stored-function deletion and routine metadata cleanup. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 drop syntax and executes as a no-op warning; routine metadata cleanup is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DROP FUNCTION` (loadable) | ⚪ | low | Loadable-function deregistration syntax. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 drop syntax and executes as a no-op warning; loadable-function deregistration is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DROP INDEX` | 🟡 | top | Standalone index removal semantics. | Implemented for `DROP INDEX name ON table` and `ALTER TABLE ... DROP INDEX` / `DROP KEY` against supported base tables, including schema/table/index validation, foreign-key dependency rejection for child supporting indexes and parent primary/unique indexes, metadata cleanup from the internal index catalog, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS`, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE`, removal from later unique-conflict checks, and MySQL-compatible implicit commit before validation/execution including failing validation and read-only transaction paths. Remaining primary-key surfaces and physical-index cleanup remain deferred. See [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md), [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `DROP LOGFILE GROUP` | ❌ | low | NDB logfile group syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `DROP PROCEDURE` | ⚪ | medium | Stored-procedure deletion and metadata cleanup. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 drop syntax and executes as a no-op warning; routine metadata cleanup is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DROP SERVER` | ❌ | low | Foreign server metadata deletion. |  |
| `DROP SPATIAL REFERENCE SYSTEM` | ❌ | medium | Spatial reference system deletion and dependency checks. |  |
| `DROP TABLE` | 🟡 | top | Multi-table drop, temporary tables, foreign-key checks, and warnings. | Implemented for base tables created by the supported `CREATE TABLE` subset, including schema-qualified/default-schema resolution, physical SQLite table removal, table/column/index catalog cleanup, `IF EXISTS` mixed existing/missing behavior, duplicate-target validation, all-or-nothing cleanup, no-op `RESTRICT`/`CASCADE`, and foreign-key parent-table checks. With `foreign_key_checks=1`, referenced parent tables are rejected unless all referencing child tables are also dropped by the same statement; with checks disabled, parent drops succeed and child FK metadata remains. Ordinary non-`TEMPORARY` `DROP TABLE` now commits an active explicit transaction before validation and execution, including failing validation paths. Temporary-table deletion is implemented for supported temporary tables: `DROP TABLE` removes a shadowing temporary table first, and `DROP TEMPORARY TABLE` never drops base tables. Warning records and exact `DROP TEMPORARY TABLE` rollback-warning fidelity are deferred. See [DROP TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/drop-table/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), and [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md). |
| `DROP TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Tablespace deletion syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `DROP UNDO TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Undo tablespace deletion syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `DROP TRIGGER` | ⚪ | high | Trigger deletion and metadata cleanup. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 drop syntax and executes as a no-op warning; trigger metadata cleanup is deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DROP VIEW` | ❌ | high | Multi-view drop and warnings. |  |
| `RENAME TABLE` | 🟡 | top | Atomic multi-table rename semantics. | Implemented for supported MyLite base tables, including multi-pair parser/AST coverage, selected-schema and schema-qualified name resolution, cross-schema moves, left-to-right validation, existing-target/missing-source/missing-schema diagnostics, physical SQLite table renames, table/column/index/foreign-key catalog rewrites, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES`, `COLUMNS`, `STATISTICS`, FK metadata updates, generated-pattern FK constraint-name rewrites, all-or-nothing statement atomicity, and MySQL-compatible implicit commit before validation/execution including failing validation and read-only transaction cases. Temporary tables, views, triggers, privilege checks, and metadata locks remain deferred. See [RENAME TABLE spec](docs/specs/rename-table/specs.md) and [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md). |
| `TRUNCATE TABLE` | 🟡 | top | DDL-like truncate, auto-increment reset, implicit commit, and foreign-key restrictions. | Implemented for supported MyLite base tables, including parser/AST coverage, selected-schema and schema-qualified resolution, physical row removal, metadata/index preservation, `AUTO_INCREMENT` reset, affected rows `0`, deterministic diagnostics, statement atomicity, MySQL-compatible implicit commit before validation/execution, and MySQL-compatible foreign-key restrictions for referenced parent tables. With `foreign_key_checks=1`, referenced parents are rejected with error 1701 except self-references; truncating child tables succeeds. With checks disabled, parent truncation succeeds and child rows/metadata remain. Temporary tables, triggers, locks, partitions, privileges, and Performance Schema summary-table behavior remain deferred. See [TRUNCATE TABLE spec](docs/specs/truncate-table/specs.md) and [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md). |
| Atomic DDL | ❌ | top | Atomicity and crash-safety expectations for MySQL DDL equivalents. |  |
| Implicit commit boundaries | ❌ | top | Statements that cause implicit commits before and/or after execution. | Repeated `START TRANSACTION`/`BEGIN` commits the active explicit transaction before starting a new one. Non-temporary `CREATE TABLE`, ordinary non-`TEMPORARY` `DROP TABLE`, `TRUNCATE TABLE`, standalone `CREATE INDEX` / `DROP INDEX`, `RENAME TABLE`, and `ALTER TABLE` now commit the active explicit transaction before validation/execution and leave no explicit transaction active afterward; covered DDL includes both successful mutations and failing validation paths plus read-only transaction boundaries where tested. Remaining DDL implicit-commit retrofits are deferred. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), [DROP TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/drop-table/specs.md), [TRUNCATE TABLE spec](docs/specs/truncate-table/specs.md), [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md), and [RENAME TABLE spec](docs/specs/rename-table/specs.md). |

### 1.2 Data manipulation statements

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `CALL` | ⚪ | medium | Procedure invocation, result sets, OUT/INOUT parameters, and diagnostics. | Parser placeholder accepts representative MySQL 8.4 call syntax and executes as a no-op warning; procedure lookup, result sets, and parameter binding are deferred. See [stored program parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/stored-program-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DELETE` (single-table) | 🟡 | top | Single-table delete with aliases, partitions, ORDER BY, LIMIT, LOW_PRIORITY, QUICK, and IGNORE. | Executable for one user base table created by the supported `CREATE TABLE` subset, including selected-schema and schema-qualified target resolution, aliases, parser-placeholder index hints, `WHERE`, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT row_count`, Task 25 CASE expression predicates/order keys, physical deletion, affected rows, deterministic diagnostics, rollback, default strict-mode expression warning promotion, `AUTO_INCREMENT` sequence preservation, parent-side foreign-key `RESTRICT` / `NO ACTION` / `SET DEFAULT` checks, direct `ON DELETE CASCADE` / `ON DELETE SET NULL` child-row actions while `foreign_key_checks` is enabled, and supported acyclic recursive delete referential actions through downstream `CASCADE`, `SET NULL`, and `RESTRICT` / `NO ACTION` constraints. `LOW_PRIORITY`, `QUICK`, `IGNORE`, partitions, CTEs, subqueries, broad expressions beyond the supported scalar subset, trigger/generated-column effects, and full SQL-mode warning-demotion behavior remain deferred. See [single-table DELETE spec](docs/specs/delete-single-table/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), [index hint parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/index-hint-placeholders/specs.md), and [CASE expression spec](docs/specs/case-expression/specs.md). |
| `DELETE` (multi-table) | 🟡 | high | Multi-table delete forms using FROM and USING, join semantics, and affected rows. | Implemented for supported base-table join sources using `DELETE target_list FROM ...` and `DELETE FROM target_list USING ...`, including target alias resolution, parser-placeholder index hints, joined-row matching, delete-each-target-row-once semantics, multi-target affected rows, statement rollback, MySQL-compatible rejection of `ORDER BY`/`LIMIT` in multi-table form, preflight parent-side foreign-key `RESTRICT` / `NO ACTION` / `SET DEFAULT` checks before any target row is deleted, direct `ON DELETE CASCADE` / `ON DELETE SET NULL` child-row actions while `foreign_key_checks` is enabled, supported acyclic recursive delete referential actions, and overlapping aliases for the same physical target. Partitions, CTEs, triggers, temporary tables, and full multi-table expression coverage remain deferred. See [multi-table DELETE spec](docs/specs/delete-multi-table/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), and [index hint parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/index-hint-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `DO` | ❌ | medium | Expression execution with warning and error semantics. |  |
| `HANDLER` | ❌ | low | HANDLER OPEN, READ, and CLOSE cursor-like table access. |  |

### 1.3 Transactional, locking, replication, prepared, and compound statements

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `START TRANSACTION` | 🟡 | top | Transaction start modifiers including READ WRITE, READ ONLY, and WITH CONSISTENT SNAPSHOT. | Parser, AST, and runtime support are implemented for `READ WRITE`, `READ ONLY`, duplicate same-kind characteristics, `WITH CONSISTENT SNAPSHOT` under the default repeatable-read compatibility state, repeated-start implicit commit, affected rows, no result columns, DML savepoint atomicity inside explicit transactions, and read-only DML rejection. Full isolation variables, `SET TRANSACTION`, and remaining DDL implicit-commit retrofits are deferred. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md). |
| `BEGIN` / `BEGIN WORK` | 🟡 | top | Transaction begin statement distinct from compound BEGIN ... END. | Top-level `BEGIN` and `BEGIN WORK` parse and execute as transaction starters, including repeated-begin implicit commit and affected rows `0`. Stored-program `BEGIN ... END` remains deferred to the stored-program parser surface. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md). |
| `COMMIT` | 🟡 | top | AND CHAIN, AND NO CHAIN, RELEASE, NO RELEASE, completion_type, and diagnostics. | Parser and runtime support are implemented for `COMMIT`, `COMMIT WORK`, no-active success, affected rows `0`, `AND CHAIN`, `AND NO CHAIN`, `RELEASE`, `NO RELEASE`, `AND CHAIN NO RELEASE`, `AND NO CHAIN RELEASE`, and `AND NO CHAIN NO RELEASE`. `completion_type` system-variable behavior and exact protocol disconnect mapping remain deferred. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md). |
| `ROLLBACK` | 🟡 | top | AND CHAIN, AND NO CHAIN, RELEASE, NO RELEASE, completion_type, and diagnostics. | Parser and runtime support are implemented for `ROLLBACK`, `ROLLBACK WORK`, no-active success, affected rows `0`, `AND CHAIN`, `AND NO CHAIN`, `RELEASE`, `NO RELEASE`, `AND CHAIN NO RELEASE`, `AND NO CHAIN RELEASE`, and `AND NO CHAIN NO RELEASE`. `completion_type` system-variable behavior and exact protocol disconnect mapping remain deferred. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md). |
| `SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | top | Nested savepoint creation and replacement semantics. | Parser, AST, and runtime support are implemented for no-op behavior outside active autocommit-on transactions, case-insensitive names, same-name replacement, nested savepoints, and separation from Task 21 statement-owned savepoints. `SET autocommit`, stored-program savepoint levels, and DDL implicit-commit clearing remain deferred. See [savepoints spec](docs/specs/savepoints/specs.md). |
| `ROLLBACK TO SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | top | Partial rollback semantics and errors. | Parser and runtime support are implemented for `ROLLBACK [WORK] TO [SAVEPOINT]`, target-retaining partial rollback, deletion of later savepoints, error 1305 for missing names, transaction-preserving errors, read-only transactions, and pending `AUTO_INCREMENT` preservation. `SET autocommit` and stored-program savepoint levels remain deferred. See [savepoints spec](docs/specs/savepoints/specs.md). |
| `RELEASE SAVEPOINT` | 🟡 | top | Savepoint release semantics and errors. | Parser and runtime support are implemented for target-and-later savepoint release without commit or rollback, error 1305 for missing names, and generated internal SQLite names. `SET autocommit`, status counters, and stored-program savepoint levels remain deferred. See [savepoints spec](docs/specs/savepoints/specs.md). |
| `SET TRANSACTION` | ❌ | high | Isolation level and access mode at global/session/next-transaction scope. |  |
| `LOCK INSTANCE FOR BACKUP` | ❌ | low | Backup lock syntax and embedded-compatible behavior. |  |
| `UNLOCK INSTANCE` | ❌ | low | Backup lock release syntax. |  |
| `LOCK TABLES` | ⚪ | high | READ, READ LOCAL, WRITE, aliases, and implicit commit behavior. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 `LOCK TABLE` / `LOCK TABLES` table lists with aliases and `READ [LOCAL]` / `WRITE` lock types, validates selected/explicit schemas and table existence with MySQL-compatible no-database, unknown-database, system-schema access-denied, and missing-table diagnostics, then executes as a no-op warning for valid targets; actual locks, privilege checks beyond system schemas, implicit commits, and locked-session restrictions are deferred. See [table lock placeholders spec](docs/specs/table-lock-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `UNLOCK TABLES` | ⚪ | high | Table lock release and transaction interaction. | Parser placeholder accepts MySQL 8.4 `UNLOCK TABLE` / `UNLOCK TABLES`, then executes as a no-op warning; actual lock release and transaction interactions are deferred. See [table lock placeholders spec](docs/specs/table-lock-placeholders/specs.md). |

| `XA END` | ❌ | low | XA transaction branch end. |  |

| `SET sql_log_bin` | ❌ | low | Session binary logging toggle and privilege semantics. |  |

| `CHANGE REPLICATION SOURCE TO` | ❌ | low | Source connection/channel options and diagnostics. |  |
| `RESET REPLICA` | ❌ | low | Replica metadata reset syntax. |  |

Binary protocol `COM_STMT_PREPARE`, broad marker placement edge cases, and complete metadata-before-execution behavior remain deferred. See [SQL prepared statements spec](docs/specs/sql-prepared-statements/specs.md) and [ANSI_QUOTES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/ansi-quotes-sql-mode/specs.md). |
| `EXECUTE` | 🟡 | high | Prepared-statement execution with USING variables and result metadata. | First SQL-level slice implemented for per-connection prepared statements, `USING @var` bindings, SQL-literal materialization for `NULL`, text, integer, unsigned, and approximate values, SELECT and supported DML execution using the lexical SQL modes captured at `PREPARE` time, affected rows, unknown-handler diagnostics, and delegated metadata after execution starts. Binary protocol execution and complete type/coercion edge cases remain deferred. See [SQL prepared statements spec](docs/specs/sql-prepared-statements/specs.md), [ANSI_QUOTES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/ansi-quotes-sql-mode/specs.md), and [user-variable foundation spec](docs/specs/user-variable-foundation/specs.md). |
| `DEALLOCATE PREPARE` / `DROP PREPARE` | 🟡 | high | Prepared statement cleanup. | SQL-level deallocation is implemented for the per-connection prepared statement registry, including unknown-handler diagnostics and `DROP PREPARE` synonym parsing. Binary protocol lifecycle behavior remains deferred. See [SQL prepared statements spec](docs/specs/sql-prepared-statements/specs.md). |

See [table maintenance statements spec](docs/specs/table-maintenance-statements/specs.md). |

See [table maintenance statements spec](docs/specs/table-maintenance-statements/specs.md). |

| `INSTALL PLUGIN` | ❌ | low | Plugin installation syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `UNINSTALL PLUGIN` | ❌ | low | Plugin uninstallation syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `CLONE` | ❌ | low | Local and remote clone syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `SET` | 🟡 | top | Variable assignment, user variables, system variables, persisted variables, names, charset, and transaction forms. | `SET NAMES`, `SET CHARACTER SET`, session/local/global `SET sql_mode`, session/local/global `SET group_concat_max_len`, session/global `SET default_collation_for_utf8mb4`, focused direct session assignment for `character_set_client`, `character_set_results`, and `collation_connection`, and the first `SET @user_variable` assignment slice are implemented for the currently supported connection-state surfaces, including `DEFAULT`, low-value clamping/warnings for `group_concat_max_len`, MySQL-compatible deprecation and invalid-value diagnostics for `default_collation_for_utf8mb4`, the focused session-scope `REPLACE(@@SESSION.sql_mode, 'ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY', '')` removal idiom, statement-start multi-assignment evaluation for user variables, and expression reads of user variables. Persisted variables, mutable global variables beyond the current slices, arbitrary direct system-variable assignment beyond current slices, and transaction forms are deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md), [SQL mode and ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY spec](docs/specs/sql-mode-only-full-group-by/specs.md), [`group_concat_max_len` system variable spec](docs/specs/group-concat-max-len-variable/specs.md), and [user-variable foundation spec](docs/specs/user-variable-foundation/specs.md). |
| `SET CHARACTER SET` | 🟡 | top | Connection character-set shorthand semantics. | Implemented for `utf8mb4`, `utf8mb3` / `utf8`, `latin1`, `binary`, and `DEFAULT`, with `utf8` normalized to `utf8mb3` plus warning 3719, handle-owned session state, selected-schema/default-schema collation behavior, and first-slice `SHOW VARIABLES` exposure. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md) and [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md). |
| `SET NAMES` | 🟡 | top | Connection character set and collation semantics. | Implemented for `utf8mb4`, `utf8mb3` / `utf8`, `latin1`, `binary`, optional compatible `COLLATE`, and `DEFAULT`, with `utf8` normalized to `utf8mb3` plus warning 3719, MySQL-runtime-verified unknown/incompatible value behavior, and first-slice `SHOW VARIABLES` exposure; numeric error-code exposure remains deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md) and [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md). |
| `CACHE INDEX` | ❌ | low | MyISAM key cache assignment syntax. |  |
| `FLUSH` | ❌ | medium | FLUSH variants for logs, tables, privileges, status, hosts, optimizer costs, and user resources. |  |
| `KILL` | ❌ | medium | Connection/query kill syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `LOAD INDEX INTO CACHE` | ❌ | low | MyISAM index preload syntax. |  |
| `RESET` | ❌ | medium | RESET variants for source/replica/persist-style operations exposed by MySQL 8.4. |  |
| `RESET PERSIST` | ❌ | low | Persisted system variable reset syntax. |  |
| `RESTART` | ❌ | low | Server restart syntax and embedded-compatible diagnostics. |  |
| `SHUTDOWN` | ❌ | low | Server shutdown syntax and embedded-compatible diagnostics. |  |
| `DESCRIBE` / `DESC` | 🟡 | top | Table, column, and statement description semantics. | First table-description slice is implemented for supported persistent base tables, including `DESCRIBE`, `DESC`, schema-qualified names, optional column/wild filtering with identifier and string-literal arguments, MySQL-compatible six-column `SHOW COLUMNS` shape, MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, catalog-backed values, and selected-schema, missing-schema, missing-table, and unknown-system-table diagnostics. `information_schema` table descriptions are parsed but currently return an unsupported diagnostic, matching the current `SHOW COLUMNS` system-view policy. Query-plan `DESCRIBE`/`DESC` forms parse and execute as no-op parser placeholders with warning `1235`; real plan output remains deferred. See [DESCRIBE / DESC table metadata spec](docs/specs/describe-table/specs.md) and [EXPLAIN statement parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/explain-statement-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `EXPLAIN` | 🟡 | high | Explain SELECT/TABLE/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, formats, ANALYZE, and FOR CONNECTION. | The table-description synonym form `EXPLAIN tbl_name [col_name \| wild]` is implemented through the `DESCRIBE` table metadata slice. Query-plan `EXPLAIN` for `SELECT`, `TABLE`, supported DML parser forms, `FORMAT`, `ANALYZE`, `INTO`, schema scoping, and numeric `FOR CONNECTION` parses and executes as a no-op parser placeholder with warning `1235`; real plan output, analysis execution, and connection inspection remain deferred. See [DESCRIBE / DESC table metadata spec](docs/specs/describe-table/specs.md) and [EXPLAIN statement parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/explain-statement-placeholders/specs.md). |
| `HELP` | ❌ | low | Server help lookup result-set semantics. |  |
| `USE` | 🟡 | top | Default schema selection in the embedded single-file model. | Implemented as handle-owned session state over the MyLite schema catalog; schema-qualified object execution and privilege checks are deferred. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md). |

### 1.5 SHOW statements

| SHOW statement | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `SHOW BINARY LOG STATUS` | ❌ | low | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW BINARY LOGS` | ❌ | low | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW BINLOG EVENTS` | ❌ | low | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CHARACTER SET` / `SHOW CHARSET` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | First executable slice exposes the supported MyLite charset registry (`binary`, `latin1`, `utf8mb3`, `utf8mb4`) through `SHOW {CHARACTER SET\|CHARSET}` plus the MySQL-runtime-accepted `SHOW CHAR SET` spelling, with exact `Charset` / `Description` / `Default collation` / numeric `Maxlen` columns, MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, case-insensitive `LIKE` filtering with backslash escapes, `WHERE` filtering over displayed-column predicates, arithmetic, and selected scalar functions, unknown-column diagnostics, and nondiagnostic clearing. `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHARACTER_SETS` shares the same registry through its wildcard-selection slice. Full MySQL charset catalog, broader information-schema query forms, privilege behavior, and CASE, CAST, subquery, and broader WHERE expressions remain deferred. See [SHOW CHARACTER SET spec](docs/specs/show-character-set/specs.md), [SHOW WHERE filtering spec](docs/specs/show-where-filtering/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHARACTER_SETS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-character-sets/specs.md). |
| `SHOW COLLATION` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | First executable slice exposes the supported MyLite collation registry (`binary`, `latin1_bin`, `latin1_swedish_ci`, `utf8mb3_bin`, `utf8mb3_general_ci`, `utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci`, `utf8mb4_bin`, `utf8mb4_general_ci`, `utf8mb4_unicode_520_ci`, `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`) with exact `Collation` / `Charset` / numeric `Id` / `Default` / `Compiled` / numeric `Sortlen` / `Pad_attribute` columns, MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, MySQL-verified default flags, sort lengths, pad attributes, case-insensitive `LIKE` filtering with backslash escapes, `WHERE` filtering over displayed-column predicates, arithmetic, and selected scalar functions, unknown-column diagnostics, and nondiagnostic clearing. `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATIONS` shares the same registry through its wildcard-selection slice. Full MySQL collation catalog, privilege behavior, and CASE, CAST, subquery, and broader WHERE expressions remain deferred. See [SHOW COLLATION spec](docs/specs/show-collation/specs.md), [SHOW WHERE filtering spec](docs/specs/show-where-filtering/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATIONS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-collations/specs.md). |
| `SHOW COLUMNS` / `SHOW FIELDS` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | First executable slice targets supported MyLite base and temporary tables, including parser/AST coverage for `SHOW [EXTENDED] [FULL] {COLUMNS\|FIELDS} {FROM\|IN} tbl_name [{FROM\|IN} db_name] [LIKE pattern \| WHERE expr]`, `FIELDS` synonym support, selected-schema, explicit-schema, and `db.table` target resolution, MySQL-compatible non-`FULL` and `FULL` result column names and MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, catalog-backed type/null/key/default/extra/collation/privilege/comment values, temporary-table shadowing, `LIKE` filtering with backslash escapes, `WHERE` filtering over displayed-column predicates, arithmetic, and selected scalar functions, `EXTENDED` no-op behavior for the current catalog, and deterministic no-selected-schema, missing-schema, missing-table, and unknown-column diagnostics. `information_schema` system-view column descriptions are parsed but return clear unsupported diagnostics until system-view metadata lands. Hidden storage-engine columns, user views, generated invisible primary key settings, privilege filtering, locks, counters, broader CASE/CAST/subquery WHERE expressions, and broader metadata behavior remain deferred. See [SHOW COLUMNS spec](docs/specs/show-columns/specs.md), [SHOW WHERE filtering spec](docs/specs/show-where-filtering/specs.md), and [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md). |
| `SHOW COUNT(*) ERRORS` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | Implemented as part of the first diagnostic `SHOW ERRORS` slice, returning a one-row `@@session.error_count` result with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors for stored error conditions while preserving the diagnostics area. `LIKE`/`WHERE` and `LIMIT` are syntax errors for this count form, matching MySQL. SQL `@@error_count` expression reads now return the current nondiagnostic-cleared statement value for the supported system-variable slice. Complete MySQL error-code/SQLSTATE propagation, mutable `max_error_count`, full generated-condition counter semantics, and `GET DIAGNOSTICS` remain deferred. See [SHOW diagnostics spec](docs/specs/show-diagnostics/specs.md) and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| `SHOW COUNT(*) WARNINGS` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | Implemented as part of the first diagnostic `SHOW WARNINGS` slice, returning a one-row `@@session.warning_count` result with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors for stored warning, error, and note conditions while preserving the diagnostics area. `LIKE`/`WHERE` and `LIMIT` are syntax errors for this count form, matching MySQL. SQL `@@warning_count` expression reads now return the current nondiagnostic-cleared statement value for the supported system-variable slice. Broader note generation, mutable `sql_notes`/`max_error_count`, full generated-condition counter semantics, and `GET DIAGNOSTICS` remain deferred. See [SHOW diagnostics spec](docs/specs/show-diagnostics/specs.md) and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| `SHOW CREATE DATABASE` / `SHOW CREATE SCHEMA` | 🟡 | high | Result-set shape and metadata formatting for schemas. | First executable slice is implemented for MyLite schema catalog rows, including `IF NOT EXISTS` display formatting, exact `Database` / `Create Database` result columns, MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, catalog-backed charset/collation/encryption rendering, system-schema rows, backtick identifier quoting, no selected-schema requirement, and unknown-database diagnostics. Privilege filtering, counters, warning records, and `sql_quote_show_create = 0` remain deferred; unsupported `LIKE` and `WHERE` suffixes are syntactically rejected. See [SHOW CREATE DATABASE spec](docs/specs/show-create-database/specs.md). |
| `SHOW CREATE EVENT` | ❌ | medium | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CREATE FUNCTION` | ❌ | medium | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CREATE PROCEDURE` | ❌ | medium | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CREATE TABLE` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape and metadata formatting for base tables. | First executable slice is implemented for supported persistent and temporary MyLite base tables, including selected-schema and schema-qualified targets, two-column `Table` / `Create Table` result shape with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors, catalog-backed deterministic CREATE text for the supported column, generated-default, generated-column, primary-key, unique-key, nonunique-key, foreign-key, CHECK-constraint, visibility, comments, charset/collation, auto-increment, table-option subset, temporary-table shadowing with `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE` output, backtick identifier quoting, and selected-schema, missing-schema, missing-table, known-system-table, and unknown-system-table diagnostics. Views, partitions, functional indexes, storage-engine-specific options, privilege filtering, `SHOW CREATE VIEW`, and `sql_quote_show_create = 0` remain deferred. See [SHOW CREATE TABLE spec](docs/specs/show-create-table/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), and [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md). |
| `SHOW CREATE TRIGGER` | ❌ | high | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CREATE USER` | ❌ | medium | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW CREATE VIEW` | ❌ | high | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. |  |
| `SHOW DATABASES` | 🟡 | top | Result-set shape, filtering, LIKE/WHERE clauses where supported, privileges, and MySQL 8.4 deprecation/removal behavior. | `SHOW DATABASES` / `SHOW SCHEMAS` returns MyLite catalog rows with a `Database` column, supports `LIKE` filtering with MySQL-compatible `Database (pattern)` column labels, supports `WHERE` filtering over the displayed `Database` column, and attaches MySQL 8.4.9-compatible result-column descriptors. Privilege filtering and `skip_show_database` behavior are deferred. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md). |
| `SHOW ENGINE` | 🟡 | low | Generic SHOW ENGINE syntax, subcommand dispatch, result-set shape, and engine-specific diagnostics. | First executable slice implements `SHOW ENGINE engine STATUS` dispatch with MySQL-compatible result metadata, an embedded InnoDB status row, metadata-compatible empty rows for known empty-status engines, and unknown-engine diagnostics. `SHOW ENGINE ... MUTEX` and `SHOW ENGINE ... LOGS` remain deferred. See [SHOW ENGINE STATUS spec](docs/specs/show-engine-status/specs.md). |

## 2. Query expressions and SELECT semantics

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Query expression grammar | 🟡 | top | Parenthesized query expressions, query terms, and query primary rules. | Implemented for top-level query expressions over supported `SELECT`, `TABLE`, and `VALUES` operands, including `UNION`, `INTERSECT`, `EXCEPT`, MySQL-compatible `INTERSECT` precedence, parenthesized query expressions with local `ORDER BY`/`LIMIT`, and global `ORDER BY`/`LIMIT`/`OFFSET`. CTEs parse as placeholders, while derived query expressions, query expressions in subqueries, DML query sources, and optimizer behavior remain deferred. See [query expression forms spec](docs/specs/query-expression-forms/specs.md) and [UNION query expressions spec](docs/specs/union-query-expressions/specs.md). |
| `WITH` common table expressions | ⚪ | high | Non-recursive CTEs, column lists, name shadowing, and scope. | Parser placeholder accepts representative non-recursive CTE query-expression syntax and executes as a no-op warning; CTE name resolution, derived row sources, materialization, and DML CTE execution are deferred. See [query expression forms spec](docs/specs/query-expression-forms/specs.md). |
| `WITH RECURSIVE` | ⚪ | high | Recursive CTE execution, cycle behavior, limits, and type inference. | Parser placeholder accepts representative recursive CTE syntax and executes as a no-op warning; recursive evaluation, termination, type inference, and materialization are deferred. See [query expression forms spec](docs/specs/query-expression-forms/specs.md). |
| Projection list | 🟡 | top | Expression aliases, wildcard expansion, qualified wildcards, duplicate names, and metadata. | Implemented for direct column-reference projection aliases, supported Task 16 table-backed projection expressions and literals, Task 24 pure scalar, random and generated identifier, and session-information function expressions, Task 25 CASE expressions, Task 25 `COUNT`/`SUM`/`AVG`/`MIN`/`MAX` aggregate outputs, Task 29 uncorrelated scalar, `EXISTS`, scalar `IN`/`NOT IN`, scalar quantified-comparison, row scalar-comparison, row `IN`/`NOT IN`, and row quantified alias subquery expressions, duplicate output labels, one-table wildcards, Task 26 base-table inner/comma join `*`, qualified wildcard expansion, and `USING` coalesced columns, and Task 27 outer join wildcard/coalesced output, with public label/schema/table/origin accessors and Task 23 type, flag, length, decimals, charset, and nullability descriptors. Remaining function families, correlated subqueries, broader quantified subquery shapes, set-operation projection merging beyond the current top-level `UNION` slice, and projection behavior for deferred join surfaces remain deferred. See [table-backed SELECT core spec](docs/specs/select-table-core/specs.md), [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md), [result metadata and expression labels spec](docs/specs/result-metadata-expression-labels/specs.md), [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md), [session information functions spec](docs/specs/session-information-functions/specs.md), [CASE expression spec](docs/specs/case-expression/specs.md), [aggregate functions and grouping spec](docs/specs/aggregate-grouping/specs.md), [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md), [outer joins spec](docs/specs/outer-joins/specs.md), [subqueries spec](docs/specs/subqueries/specs.md), [subquery `IN` predicates spec](docs/specs/subquery-in-predicates/specs.md), [row subquery predicates spec](docs/specs/row-subquery-predicates/specs.md), [quantified subquery comparisons spec](docs/specs/quantified-subquery-comparisons/specs.md), and [row quantified subquery comparisons spec](docs/specs/row-quantified-subquery-comparisons/specs.md). |
| Table references | 🟡 | top | Base tables, aliases, schema qualifiers, derived tables, table functions, and parentheses. | Implemented for user base tables with selected-schema or schema-qualified resolution, optional `AS`/bare table aliases, parser-placeholder index hints, Task 26/27 multi-table SELECT row sources using inner/cross/comma and `LEFT`/`RIGHT` outer joins, and parenthesized nested join operands for the covered SELECT/joined-update surface. Derived tables, table functions, and partitions remain deferred. See [table-backed SELECT core spec](docs/specs/select-table-core/specs.md), [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md), [outer joins spec](docs/specs/outer-joins/specs.md), and [index hint parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/index-hint-placeholders/specs.md). |
| Inner joins | 🟡 | top | JOIN, INNER JOIN, CROSS JOIN, ON, and USING. | Implemented for SELECT over user base tables, including aliases, parser-placeholder index hints, explicit join operand scoping, parenthesized nested join operands, optional `ON` predicates over the supported scalar expression subset, optional `USING` column validation/coalescing/name resolution, wildcard expansion, result metadata, and WHERE/ORDER/LIMIT/aggregate interaction. Natural joins, STRAIGHT_JOIN, derived tables, and optimizer behavior remain deferred. See [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md) and [index hint parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/index-hint-placeholders/specs.md). |
| Comma joins | 🟡 | top | Comma-separated table references, Cartesian products, and precedence with explicit joins. | Implemented for SELECT over user base tables, including Cartesian products, WHERE filtering, lower comma precedence relative to explicit joins, and `ON` visibility diagnostics for comma-left tables outside an explicit join operand. Parenthesized join groups and optimizer behavior remain deferred. See [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md). |
| Outer joins | 🟡 | top | LEFT/RIGHT OUTER JOIN null-extension and predicate placement. | Implemented for SELECT over user base tables, including `LEFT`/`RIGHT` with optional `OUTER`, required `ON` or `USING`, parenthesized nested join operands, null extension of the non-preserved side, `ON` before null extension and `WHERE` after it, aliases, parser-placeholder index hints, explicit join operand scoping, `USING` validation/coalescing/name resolution, wildcard expansion, result metadata nullability, warning-count preservation, and ORDER/LIMIT/aggregate grouping interaction. | Natural joins | ❌ | high | NATURAL INNER/LEFT/RIGHT JOIN column matching and metadata. |  |
| `STRAIGHT_JOIN` | ❌ | medium | Join-order forcing syntax and optimizer interaction. |  |
| Lateral derived tables | ❌ | medium | LATERAL derived table correlation rules. |  |
| `WHERE` | 🟡 | top | Predicate semantics, type conversion, three-valued logic, and short-circuit-sensitive warnings. | Implemented for one user base table in table-backed `SELECT`, over the Task 16 expression subset, Task 24 supported scalar function subset, Task 25 CASE expressions, and Task 29 uncorrelated scalar/`EXISTS`/scalar `IN`/`NOT IN`/scalar quantified-comparison/row scalar-comparison/row `IN`/`NOT IN`/row quantified alias subqueries, with MyLite-owned row scanning, predicate name resolution, three-valued filtering, conversion and division warnings, invalid `ESCAPE` diagnostics, warning lifecycle, and projection metadata preservation. Task 26/27 also apply the supported predicate subset after base-table join production and outer-join null extension, with `ON` predicates scoped to explicit join operands and evaluated before null extension. Supported information-schema system views use the same predicate runtime. Single-table `UPDATE` and `DELETE` predicates use the same supported scalar subset without subquery execution. The scalar `FROM DUAL WHERE` subset is supported for standalone DUAL selects and `INSERT ... SELECT FROM DUAL`, including false-predicate projection suppression. Correlated subqueries, broader quantified subquery shapes, remaining function families, broad collations, no-table predicates without `FROM DUAL`, optimizer pushdown, index use, and predicate behavior for deferred join surfaces remain deferred. See [WHERE clause spec](docs/specs/where-clause/specs.md), [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md), [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md), [CASE expression spec](docs/specs/case-expression/specs.md), [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md), [outer joins spec](docs/specs/outer-joins/specs.md), [composable information-schema SELECT spec](docs/specs/information-schema-composable-select/specs.md), [subqueries spec](docs/specs/subqueries/specs.md), [subquery `IN` predicates spec](docs/specs/subquery-in-predicates/specs.md), [row subquery predicates spec](docs/specs/row-subquery-predicates/specs.md), [quantified subquery comparisons spec](docs/specs/quantified-subquery-comparisons/specs.md), and [row quantified subquery comparisons spec](docs/specs/row-quantified-subquery-comparisons/specs.md). |

Session `sql_mode` controls whether `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` validation rejects nonaggregated hidden columns in grouped queries; clearing the mode permits the MySQL extension for currently executable query shapes. | `DISTINCT` / `DISTINCTROW` | 🟡 | top | Duplicate elimination semantics and metadata. | Implemented for no-table scalar SELECT and current table-backed SELECT surfaces, including `ALL`, repeated same-mode modifiers, mixed-mode error 1221, projected-row duplicate comparison, `NULL` duplicate collapsing, supported collation-sensitive text comparison, metadata preservation, joins, aggregates, `ORDER BY`/`LIMIT`, and DISTINCT/order error 3065 for hidden non-selected columns when `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` is active. Clearing `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` permits ordering by non-selected columns for currently executable query shapes. Top-level set operations support default and explicit `DISTINCT` duplicate modes for `UNION`, `INTERSECT`, and `EXCEPT`. Aggregate-local `DISTINCT`, derived tables, subqueries, optimizer pushdown, and deferred row sources remain deferred.
