## 3. DDL detail surface

### 3.1 CREATE TABLE, index, and constraint features

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Column definition grammar | 🟡 | top | Type, nullability, defaults, visibility, comments, storage, format, references, and constraints. | Supported `CREATE TABLE` column definitions now execute for integer, boolean, string, binary, exact numeric, approximate numeric, and temporal column types plus common column attributes, writing SQLite storage and MyLite column metadata. `SERIAL` and `SERIAL DEFAULT VALUE` aliases execute for supported create-table paths, normalizing to MySQL-shaped integer `AUTO_INCREMENT` and unique-index metadata. Generated columns, references, checks, physical default evaluation, physical constraint enforcement, full semantic validation, and warning records are deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md), [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md), [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md), [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md), [column attributes spec](docs/specs/column-attributes/specs.md), [primary keys and AUTO_INCREMENT spec](docs/specs/primary-keys-auto-increment/specs.md), [SERIAL column aliases spec](docs/specs/serial-column-alias/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE unique and secondary indexes spec](docs/specs/create-table-indexes/specs.md), and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Silent column specification changes | ❌ | high | MySQL automatic rewrites of column specifications and SHOW CREATE output. |  |
| Default expressions | 🟡 | top | Literal and expression defaults, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP variants, and error cases. | `CREATE TABLE` records supported literal, parenthesized-expression, targeted `CURRENT_TIMESTAMP[(fsp)]`, and MySQL-normalized `NOW[(fsp)]` default text in column metadata, including `DEFAULT_GENERATED` for generated defaults. `SHOW CREATE TABLE` renders supported generated defaults as parenthesized expressions rather than string literals. `INSERT ... VALUES` and `INSERT ... SET` evaluate deterministic null, literal, unary numeric, string, current-timestamp/`NOW()`, and constant parenthesized generated defaults in the existing scalar expression subset. Other function-call generated defaults remain deferred and fail deterministically when needed during insert. Type-specific conversion diagnostics, subqueries, variables, and warning records are deferred. See [column attributes spec](docs/specs/column-attributes/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), [INSERT ... VALUES spec](docs/specs/insert-values/specs.md), [generated default expressions spec](docs/specs/generated-default-expressions/specs.md), and [INSERT ... SET spec](docs/specs/insert-set/specs.md). |
| Generated columns | ❌ | high | Virtual/stored generated columns, dependencies, indexes, and metadata. |  |
| Invisible columns | 🟡 | medium | Implicit column lists, SELECT * behavior, and metadata flags. | `CREATE TABLE` records `VISIBLE` and `INVISIBLE` column attributes, exposes invisible columns through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS.EXTRA` and `SHOW COLUMNS` / `SHOW FIELDS` `Extra`, omits invisible columns from Task 15 wildcard SELECT expansion, and allows explicit invisible-column selection. All-invisible table validation, implicit column-list behavior, and `SHOW CREATE TABLE` formatting remain deferred. See [column attributes spec](docs/specs/column-attributes/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), [table-backed SELECT core spec](docs/specs/select-table-core/specs.md), and [SHOW COLUMNS spec](docs/specs/show-columns/specs.md). |
| Column comments | 🟡 | high | Column `COMMENT` attributes, metadata, length checks, and SHOW output. | `CREATE TABLE` records supported column `COMMENT 'string'` attributes into column metadata and exposes them through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS.COLUMN_COMMENT` and `SHOW FULL COLUMNS` / `SHOW FULL FIELDS` `Comment`. Length validation, escaping normalization, and warning records remain deferred. See [column attributes spec](docs/specs/column-attributes/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), and [SHOW COLUMNS spec](docs/specs/show-columns/specs.md). |
| Column storage/format attributes | ⚪ | low | NDB-oriented `COLUMN_FORMAT` and `STORAGE` column attributes and diagnostics. | Parser accepts `COLUMN_FORMAT DEFAULT/FIXED/DYNAMIC` and `STORAGE DEFAULT/DISK/MEMORY` in parse-only column definitions and rejects malformed syntax such as `=` forms or unknown values; engine-specific effects, warnings, and `SHOW CREATE TABLE` formatting are deferred. See [column attributes spec](docs/specs/column-attributes/specs.md). |
| Generated invisible primary keys | ❌ | low | MySQL-generated invisible primary key behavior, metadata, and dump/replication interactions. |  |
| AUTO_INCREMENT columns | 🟡 | top | Allocation, persistence, lock modes, explicit values, and overflow behavior. | Inline `AUTO_INCREMENT`, `SERIAL` / `SERIAL DEFAULT VALUE` aliases, and table-level `AUTO_INCREMENT` execute into metadata for supported `CREATE TABLE` statements. `INSERT ... VALUES` and `INSERT ... SET` allocate omitted, `NULL`, `0`, and `DEFAULT` auto values in the default SQL mode, store explicit `0` when `NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO` is active, advance after accepted explicit high values, persist consumed generated values after failed statement rollback, persist the next catalog value, and expose the first generated id through `mylite_last_insert_id()`; `INSERT ... VALUES` also reserves simple multi-row generated intervals. Overflow fidelity, lock modes, full type/key/default semantic diagnostics, and warning records remain deferred. See [primary keys and AUTO_INCREMENT spec](docs/specs/primary-keys-auto-increment/specs.md), [SERIAL column aliases spec](docs/specs/serial-column-alias/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), [INSERT ... VALUES spec](docs/specs/insert-values/specs.md), [INSERT ... SET spec](docs/specs/insert-set/specs.md), and [NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO SQL mode spec](docs/specs/no-auto-value-on-zero/specs.md). |
| Primary keys | 🟡 | top | Definition forms, implicit NOT NULL behavior, metadata, and errors. | Inline `PRIMARY KEY`, inline `KEY`, and table-level `PRIMARY KEY` constraints now execute into column and statistics metadata, including duplicate-primary diagnostics and implicit metadata `NOT NULL`. `INSERT ... VALUES` enforces deterministic primary/unique duplicate checks for inserted values. Full physical index storage, type/prefix suitability checks, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, and storage-engine warnings are deferred. See [primary keys and AUTO_INCREMENT spec](docs/specs/primary-keys-auto-increment/specs.md), [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md), and [INSERT ... VALUES spec](docs/specs/insert-values/specs.md). |
| Unique indexes | 🟡 | top | NULL handling, prefix lengths, functional key parts, and conflict semantics. | Inline `UNIQUE`/`UNIQUE KEY` and supported table-level unique indexes now execute into statistics metadata with deterministic generated names and duplicate explicit-name checks. Physical uniqueness enforcement, functional key parts, full prefix suitability checks, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, and warning records are deferred. See [CREATE TABLE unique and secondary indexes spec](docs/specs/create-table-indexes/specs.md) and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Nonunique indexes | 🟡 | top | BTREE/HASH clauses, visibility, comments, and parser options. | Supported table-level `KEY` and `INDEX` forms now execute into statistics metadata with index names, key parts, prefix lengths, order, visibility, type, and comments. Physical SQLite index creation, optimizer behavior, storage-engine diagnostics, and warning records are deferred. See [CREATE TABLE unique and secondary indexes spec](docs/specs/create-table-indexes/specs.md) and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Descending indexes | 🟡 | high | DESC key-part syntax and ordering semantics. | `ASC` and `DESC` key-part markers are exposed through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS.COLLATION` for supported `CREATE TABLE` indexes; physical order and optimizer behavior are deferred. See [CREATE TABLE unique and secondary indexes spec](docs/specs/create-table-indexes/specs.md) and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Prefix indexes | 🟡 | top | Prefix length parsing, byte/character semantics, and limits. | Integer prefix lengths are exposed through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS.SUB_PART` for supported `CREATE TABLE` indexes; type suitability, byte/character semantics, storage limits, and warnings are deferred. See [CREATE TABLE unique and secondary indexes spec](docs/specs/create-table-indexes/specs.md) and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Functional key parts | ❌ | high | Expression key parts and metadata. |  |
| Multi-valued indexes | ❌ | medium | JSON array multi-valued index syntax, casts, optimizer behavior, and metadata. |  |
| FULLTEXT indexes | 🟡 | high | Index DDL, parser options, MATCH metadata, and embedded search behavior. | Standalone `CREATE FULLTEXT INDEX` and `ALTER TABLE ... ADD FULLTEXT INDEX` are implemented as metadata-backed index DDL for supported character/text columns, including `WITH PARSER` name preservation, `COMMENT`, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS` rows with `INDEX_TYPE='FULLTEXT'`, `COLLATION=NULL`, prefix lengths ignored as MySQL does, `SHOW INDEX`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, warning 124 for the first full-text index on a table, MySQL error 1795 for multiple full-text creations in one ALTER, and MySQL-compatible diagnostics for non-text columns and explicit key-part order. Full-text search, `MATCH ... AGAINST`, parser-plugin tokenization, and table-definition `FULLTEXT KEY` remain deferred. See [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md) and [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| SPATIAL indexes | ❌ | medium | Geometry column requirements and spatial metadata. |  |
| Foreign keys | 🟡 | high | Definition, matching, cascades, restrict/no action, set null/default, checks, and metadata. | Table-level `CREATE TABLE ... FOREIGN KEY` definitions are cataloged for supported persistent base tables with generated constraint names, supporting child index creation/reuse, referenced unique-key validation, `foreign_key_checks=0` missing-parent metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE`, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS`, and `SHOW CREATE TABLE` output. `ALTER TABLE ... ADD/DROP FOREIGN KEY` is implemented for supported persistent base tables, including FK-only statements and supported mixed column/index ALTER statements, with existing-child-row validation when `foreign_key_checks=1`, missing-parent metadata when checks are disabled, supporting child index creation/reuse, catalog cleanup, statement-atomic shadow-table rebuilds, and MySQL-observed affected-row counts for covered shapes. Child-row enforcement is implemented for supported `INSERT ... VALUES`, `INSERT ... SET`, `INSERT ... SELECT FROM DUAL`, `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`, `INSERT IGNORE`, `REPLACE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and joined `UPDATE` paths, including `foreign_key_checks`, `NULL` child parts, simple self-references, warning demotion for `INSERT IGNORE` and single-table `UPDATE IGNORE`, and unchanged orphan-key updates after checks are re-enabled. Parent-side `RESTRICT`, `NO ACTION`, and MySQL-observed `SET DEFAULT` checks are implemented for supported parent key UPDATEs, DELETEs, multi-table DELETE preflight, self-references, and REPLACE conflict deletes, with single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` demoting covered parent-update violations to warning 1451 and skipping the row. Direct `ON UPDATE CASCADE`, `ON UPDATE SET NULL`, `ON DELETE CASCADE`, and `ON DELETE SET NULL` actions are implemented for supported UPDATE/DELETE paths and REPLACE conflict deletes, recurse through supported acyclic downstream `CASCADE`, `SET NULL`, and `RESTRICT` / `NO ACTION` chains, and are skipped while `foreign_key_checks=0`; self-referential parent update actions reject with error 1451 in the MySQL-observed shape. Foreign-key dependency checks reject dropping child supporting indexes and parent primary/unique indexes with error 1553, reject dropping child/parent foreign-key columns with errors 1828/1829, reject incompatible FK column definition changes with error 3780, reject dropping referenced parent tables with surviving children while checks are enabled, allow parent+child multi-table drops, preserve child FK metadata for checks-off parent drops, rewrite child/parent FK metadata on table and column renames, reject temporary child foreign keys, treat temporary parent tables as unavailable to persistent child foreign keys, execute supported mixed-action FK ALTER statements atomically with column/index changes, and reject referenced parent `TRUNCATE TABLE` with error 1701 while checks are enabled except self-references. Exact diagnostics remain deferred. See [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md). |
| CHECK constraints | 🟡 | high | Expression validation, enforcement, names, and metadata. | Inline and table-level `CREATE TABLE ... CHECK` definitions are cataloged for supported persistent and temporary base tables, exposed through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS` and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, rendered in `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, and enforced for the covered row-local expression subset across supported `INSERT`, `INSERT ... SET`, ODKU, `REPLACE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and joined `UPDATE` paths. CHECK failures use MySQL error/warning 3819; `INSERT IGNORE` and `UPDATE IGNORE` skip covered violating rows. `NOT ENFORCED` metadata is preserved and not enforced. `CREATE TABLE` validates duplicate generated/explicit CHECK names with error 3822 and validates the first deterministic row-local CHECK expression slice, including later column references, qualified local references, unsupported function/subquery/variable/unknown-column diagnostics, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` reference rejection. `ALTER TABLE ... ADD/DROP/ALTER CHECK` is implemented for supported persistent and temporary base tables, including ADD CHECK expression validation and CHECK actions mixed with supported column, index, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` table-option actions. Generated-column dependency handling, broader MySQL expression semantics, and broader expression formatting remain deferred. See [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), [SHOW CREATE TABLE spec](docs/specs/show-create-table/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-check-constraints/specs.md). |
| Constraint naming | ❌ | top | Generated names, duplicate handling, schema scope, and SHOW CREATE output. |  |
| Table options: engine | 🟡 | top | ENGINE, SECONDARY_ENGINE, engine attributes, and unsupported-engine diagnostics. | `CREATE TABLE` accepts and executes `ENGINE [=] InnoDB`, records `ENGINE='InnoDB'`, and returns a deterministic unsupported-engine execution diagnostic for other parsed engine names. Other engine-related table options remain unsupported. See [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Table options: charset/collation | 🟡 | top | DEFAULT CHARACTER SET, COLLATE, and conversion-sensitive metadata. | `CREATE TABLE` accepts and executes `[DEFAULT] CHARACTER SET`/`CHARSET` and `[DEFAULT] COLLATE` for the initial charset/collation registry, validates unknown and mismatched values, and records effective table/column metadata. Full charset catalog coverage and conversion-sensitive storage semantics are deferred. See [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| Table options: storage | ❌ | high | ROW_FORMAT, COMPRESSION, ENCRYPTION, TABLESPACE, KEY_BLOCK_SIZE, and directories. |  |
| Table options: statistics | ❌ | high | STATS_AUTO_RECALC, STATS_PERSISTENT, STATS_SAMPLE_PAGES, AVG_ROW_LENGTH, MAX_ROWS, MIN_ROWS. |  |
| Table options: misc | 🟡 | high | AUTO_INCREMENT, COMMENT, CHECKSUM, DELAY_KEY_WRITE, INSERT_METHOD, PACK_KEYS, PASSWORD, UNION. | `CREATE TABLE` accepts and executes table `AUTO_INCREMENT [=] integer` and `COMMENT [=] 'string'` into metadata. `ALTER TABLE` accepts and executes `AUTO_INCREMENT [=] integer` for supported base tables. Broader allocation behavior, warnings, and other miscellaneous table options remain unsupported. See [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md) and [ALTER TABLE AUTO_INCREMENT spec](docs/specs/alter-table-auto-increment/specs.md). |
| NDB comment options | ❌ | low | NDB-specific table and column comment options recognized by MySQL grammar. |  |
| Temporary table metadata | 🟡 | top | Session isolation, name shadowing, and cleanup. | Implemented for supported `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE` tables through connection-local catalog rows, cross-connection invisibility, temporary-first executable and targeted metadata resolution, standalone temporary index DDL metadata for `SHOW INDEX` / `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, automatic close cleanup, omission from schema-wide `SHOW TABLES` / `SHOW FULL TABLES` / `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES`, and persistent rows for `SHOW TABLE STATUS` / `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS` when a temporary table shadows the same name. Temporary-table privileges and full temporary DDL transaction semantics remain deferred. See [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md). |
| CREATE INDEX options | 🟡 | top | ALGORITHM, LOCK, visibility, comments, and index type clauses. | Standalone `CREATE INDEX` now accepts `USING`/`TYPE` in MySQL-supported positions, `KEY_BLOCK_SIZE`, `COMMENT`, `VISIBLE`/`INVISIBLE`, engine attributes, full-text-only parser-level `WITH PARSER` syntax, and embedded no-op `ALGORITHM`/`LOCK` modifiers. Metadata-backed ordinary and unique indexes preserve comment, visibility, prefix, order, and effective BTREE type where represented; standalone full-text indexes preserve comments and parser names while reporting `INDEX_TYPE='FULLTEXT'`. Full storage-engine validation, full-text parser tokenization/search semantics, optimizer effects, and physical index selection are deferred. See [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md). |
| CREATE VIEW options | ❌ | high | ALGORITHM, DEFINER, SQL SECURITY, column list, and WITH CHECK OPTION. |  |

### 3.2 ALTER TABLE actions

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ADD COLUMN` | 🟡 | top | Positioning, FIRST/AFTER, multiple columns, generated/invisible/default handling. | Implemented for ordinary column definitions over the supported type/attribute subset, with explicit and implicit default backfill, visibility validation, dense ordinal rewrites, metadata updates, and statement atomicity. Inline key/constraint creation, unsupported generated defaults, and broader dependency checks are deferred. |
| `DROP COLUMN` | 🟡 | top | Dependency checks, generated columns, indexes, constraints, and errors. | Implemented for stored values and catalog metadata, including index key-part cleanup, empty-index removal, duplicate-index warnings after rewrites, auto-increment cleanup, dropping-every-column rejection, foreign-key child/parent column dependency errors, and atomic rollback. Generated-column, trigger, and check dependency catalogs remain deferred. |
| `RENAME COLUMN` | 🟡 | top | Metadata rewrite and dependency updates. | Implemented for column metadata, stored-value preservation, index key-part metadata updates, and child/parent foreign-key column metadata rewrites. Generated-column dependency rewrites remain deferred. |
| `CHANGE COLUMN` | 🟡 | top | Rename plus type/attribute change semantics. | Implemented for complete replacement definitions, optional rename/repositioning, metadata/storage shadow rewrite, index metadata updates, not-null validation, compatible child/parent foreign-key metadata rewrites, incompatible foreign-key column diagnostics, and affected-row reporting for copied rows. Full MySQL value conversion and later generated/check/trigger dependency surfaces remain deferred. |
| `MODIFY COLUMN` | 🟡 | top | Type/attribute change without rename. | Implemented for complete replacement definitions, optional repositioning, visibility changes, metadata/storage shadow rewrite, missing-column diagnostics, not-null validation, all-invisible rejection, and incompatible child/parent foreign-key column diagnostics. Full MySQL value conversion and later generated/check/trigger dependency surfaces remain deferred. |
| `ALTER COLUMN SET DEFAULT` | 🟡 | top | Default mutation semantics. | Implemented for supported base tables with ordinary literal defaults, metadata updates, strict/non-strict later DML behavior, invalid `NULL` defaults on `NOT NULL` columns, and MySQL-compatible hidden `AUTO_INCREMENT` defaults. Generated default expressions remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE column operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-column-operations/specs.md). |
| `ALTER COLUMN DROP DEFAULT` | 🟡 | top | Default removal semantics. | Implemented for supported base tables, including internal distinction between implicit nullable defaults and explicitly dropped defaults, MySQL-compatible strict 1364 errors and non-strict warnings for omitted values, `SHOW CREATE TABLE` formatting, and dropped `AUTO_INCREMENT` omitted-default behavior. Generated-column and broader dependency interactions remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE column operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-column-operations/specs.md). |
| `ALTER COLUMN SET VISIBLE` / `SET INVISIBLE` | ❌ | medium | Column visibility changes and restrictions. |  |
| `ADD PRIMARY KEY` | 🟡 | top | Primary key addition and data validation. | Implemented for supported MyLite base tables with existing-row duplicate/`NULL` validation, implicit `NOT NULL` column metadata rewrite, statistics metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` primary-key rows, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` key-part rows, write-path duplicate enforcement, mixed column/key atomicity, and rollback. Generated-column dependencies and foreign-key dependencies remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `DROP PRIMARY KEY` | 🟡 | top | Primary key removal and auto-increment restrictions. | Implemented for metadata-backed primary-key removal while preserving `NOT NULL` column state, rejecting `AUTO_INCREMENT` dependency, rejecting parent primary keys needed by a foreign key, and removing the `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` primary-key rows. Broader generated-column/trigger/privilege dependency checks remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `ADD UNIQUE` | 🟡 | top | Unique index addition and duplicate validation. | Implemented for supported MyLite base tables using standalone index semantics: duplicate validation, nullable unique-key behavior, generated/named index behavior, visibility, prefix parts, statistics metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` unique rows, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` key-part rows, DML conflict integration, and mixed column/key default-backfill validation. Functional/multi-valued key parts and optimizer use remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `ADD INDEX` / `ADD KEY` | 🟡 | top | Secondary index addition and metadata. | Implemented for metadata-backed nonunique index addition with generated names, key-part metadata, visibility, comments, HASH fallback warnings, duplicate-index warnings, and Task 35 mixed-action atomicity. Functional/multi-valued key parts and physical optimizer indexes remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `ADD FULLTEXT` | 🟡 | high | Full-text index addition. | Implemented as metadata-backed ALTER index DDL for supported character/text columns, including parser/comment metadata, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS`, `SHOW INDEX`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, warning 124 for the first full-text index on a table, MySQL error 1795 for multiple full-text creations in one ALTER, non-text column diagnostics, explicit-order diagnostics, and statement-atomic rollback on failure. Full-text search, parser-plugin tokenization, and physical full-text index behavior remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `ADD SPATIAL` | ⚪ | medium | Spatial index addition. | Parsed as an ALTER surface and returns a deterministic unsupported diagnostic before mutation until spatial types, SRID checks, and spatial index runtime are implemented. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `DROP INDEX` / `DROP KEY` | 🟡 | top | Index removal and constraint dependencies. | Standalone `DROP INDEX name ON table` and `ALTER TABLE DROP INDEX` / `DROP KEY` are implemented for metadata-backed indexes created by supported table/index DDL, including conflict-surface removal, foreign-key dependency rejection for child supporting indexes and parent primary/unique indexes, and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.STATISTICS`, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` metadata cleanup. Broader generated-column/trigger/privilege dependency checks remain deferred. See [standalone CREATE INDEX and DROP INDEX spec](docs/specs/create-drop-index/specs.md), [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `RENAME INDEX` / `RENAME KEY` | 🟡 | high | Index rename semantics. | Implemented as metadata-only rename of non-primary indexes while preserving key parts, visibility, uniqueness, conflict order, statistics rows, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` names, and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE` names for unique indexes. Exact primary-index rename diagnostics remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLE_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-table-constraints/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.KEY_COLUMN_USAGE spec](docs/specs/information-schema-key-column-usage/specs.md). |
| `ALTER INDEX VISIBLE` / `INVISIBLE` | 🟡 | medium | Index visibility metadata and optimizer behavior. | Implemented for metadata visibility toggles with uniqueness still enforced and explicit/implicit primary-key invisibility rejected. Optimizer effects and physical index use remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK` | 🟡 | high | Check constraint addition and validation. | Implemented for supported persistent and temporary base tables, including generated names, explicit schema-level duplicate-name diagnostics for ALTER, DDL-time rejection of unsupported functions, subqueries, variables, unknown columns, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` references, existing-row validation for enforced checks, `NOT ENFORCED` metadata, CHECK catalog rows, `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS` / `TABLE_CONSTRAINTS` exposure, `SHOW CREATE TABLE` formatting, error 3819 on existing-row violations, and CHECK actions mixed with supported column, index, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` table-option actions using statement-atomic shadow rewrites. Broader MySQL CHECK expression semantics remain deferred. See [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), [SHOW CREATE TABLE spec](docs/specs/show-create-table/specs.md), and [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `DROP CHECK` | 🟡 | high | Check constraint removal. | Implemented through `DROP CHECK` and `DROP CONSTRAINT`, including CHECK catalog cleanup, metadata disappearance from `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS` / `TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, affected rows 0, MySQL-compatible missing-name error 3821, and statement-atomic execution when mixed with supported column, index, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` table-option actions. See [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md) and [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `ALTER CHECK ENFORCED` / `NOT ENFORCED` | 🟡 | high | Check enforcement toggling. | Implemented for supported persistent and temporary base tables, including metadata toggling, existing-row validation when enabling enforcement, error 3819 on invalid existing rows, affected-row behavior for enabling from `NOT ENFORCED`, later DML enforcement changes, and statement-atomic execution when mixed with supported column, index, and `AUTO_INCREMENT` table-option actions. See [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md) and [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md). |
| `ADD CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY` | 🟡 | high | Foreign key addition, validation, indexes, and actions. | `ALTER TABLE ... ADD [CONSTRAINT] ... FOREIGN KEY` is implemented for supported persistent base tables in FK-only statements and supported mixed column/index ALTER statements. ADD validates child columns against the final ALTER model, parent primary/unique keys when the parent exists, duplicate constraint names, and existing child rows when `foreign_key_checks=1`; it creates or reuses a supporting child index, including indexes added in the same statement, writes foreign-key catalog rows, exposes metadata through `INFORMATION_SCHEMA` and `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, and reports the child table row count for covered MySQL-observed shapes. Missing-parent metadata with `foreign_key_checks=0`, enforcement by later DML, direct and supported acyclic recursive `ON UPDATE` and `ON DELETE` actions, supporting/parent index dependency checks, parent-table drop behavior, table/column rename catalog rewrites, and truncate checks use the shared foreign-key runtime. Temporary tables and exact diagnostics remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), and [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-referential-constraints/specs.md). |
| `DROP FOREIGN KEY` | 🟡 | high | Foreign key removal and metadata cleanup. | `ALTER TABLE ... DROP FOREIGN KEY` removes matching foreign-key catalog rows in FK-only and supported mixed column/index ALTER statements, leaves the supporting index in place unless a same-statement supported action drops it, reports zero affected rows for covered shapes, and returns MySQL-style missing-key diagnostics for absent names. Temporary tables and broader dependency cleanup remain deferred. See [ALTER TABLE key and constraint operations spec](docs/specs/alter-table-key-constraint-operations/specs.md) and [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md). |
| `DISABLE KEYS` / `ENABLE KEYS` | ❌ | low | MyISAM-style key maintenance syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `RENAME TO` | 🟡 | top | Table rename via ALTER TABLE. | Implemented for supported MyLite base tables through `ALTER TABLE t RENAME u`, `ALTER TABLE t RENAME TO u`, and `ALTER TABLE t RENAME AS u`, sharing the `RENAME TABLE` runtime path for schema resolution, physical renames, table/column/index/foreign-key metadata rewrites, statement atomicity, and MySQL-compatible implicit commit behavior. Temporary-table rename, mixed rename plus rebuild actions, and generated-column/trigger dependencies remain deferred. See [RENAME TABLE spec](docs/specs/rename-table/specs.md). |
| `ORDER BY` | ❌ | medium | Physical row ordering syntax and embedded-compatible behavior. |  |
| `CONVERT TO CHARACTER SET` | ❌ | high | Column/table charset and collation conversion semantics. |  |
| `DEFAULT CHARACTER SET` / `COLLATE` | ❌ | high | Table default charset/collation changes. |  |
| `FORCE` | ❌ | medium | Forced table rebuild semantics. |  |
| `DISCARD TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Tablespace discard syntax. |  |
| `IMPORT TABLESPACE` | ❌ | low | Tablespace import syntax. |  |
| `ALGORITHM` | ❌ | high | DEFAULT, INSTANT, INPLACE, COPY handling and diagnostics. |  |
| `LOCK` | ❌ | high | DEFAULT, NONE, SHARED, EXCLUSIVE handling and diagnostics. |  |
| Partition maintenance | ⚪ | low | ADD, DROP, DISCARD, IMPORT, TRUNCATE, COALESCE, REORGANIZE, EXCHANGE, ANALYZE, CHECK, OPTIMIZE, REBUILD, REPAIR, REMOVE PARTITIONING. | Parser placeholder accepts representative MySQL 8.4 partition maintenance syntax and executes as a no-op warning; storage, pruning, metadata, and per-partition maintenance are deferred. See [table partitioning parser placeholders spec](docs/specs/table-partitioning-parser-placeholders/specs.md). |

## 4. Type system, literals, and conversion

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `TINYINT` | 🟡 | top | Signed/unsigned integer range, display width compatibility, zerofill, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes signed and unsigned ranges, precision, scale, `DATA_TYPE`, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; executable DML now clips signed and unsigned out-of-range writes with strict/non-strict/`INSERT IGNORE` diagnostics for covered paths. `ZEROFILL` and broader value enforcement surfaces remain deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `SMALLINT` | 🟡 | high | Signed/unsigned integer range, display width compatibility, zerofill, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes signed and unsigned ranges, precision, scale, `DATA_TYPE`, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; executable DML now clips signed and unsigned out-of-range writes with strict/non-strict/`INSERT IGNORE` diagnostics for covered paths. `ZEROFILL` and broader value enforcement surfaces remain deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `MEDIUMINT` | 🟡 | top | Signed/unsigned integer range, display width compatibility, zerofill, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes signed and unsigned ranges, precision, scale, `DATA_TYPE`, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; executable DML now clips signed and unsigned out-of-range writes with strict/non-strict/`INSERT IGNORE` diagnostics for covered paths. `ZEROFILL` and broader value enforcement surfaces remain deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `INT` / `INTEGER` | 🟡 | top | Signed/unsigned integer range, display width compatibility, zerofill, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes `INTEGER` to `int`, signed and unsigned ranges, precision, scale, `DATA_TYPE`, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; executable DML now clips signed and unsigned out-of-range writes with strict/non-strict/`INSERT IGNORE` diagnostics for covered paths. `ZEROFILL` and broader value enforcement surfaces remain deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `BIGINT` | 🟡 | top | Signed/unsigned integer range, overflow, unsigned arithmetic, and metadata. | Internal descriptor records signed and unsigned ranges as text, including unsigned max `18446744073709551615`, and normalizes precision, scale, `DATA_TYPE`, and `COLUMN_TYPE`. Executable DML now clips signed `BIGINT` and unsigned `BIGINT` range overflows for covered write paths, stores exact `BIGINT UNSIGNED` values above `INT64_MAX` as canonical decimal text, generates and advances `BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT` values above signed 64-bit range with exact metadata, and preserves strict/non-strict/`IGNORE` diagnostics for verified direct, quoted, and `CAST(... AS UNSIGNED)` assignments. Broad unsigned arithmetic, `ZEROFILL`, and comparison/index ordering over text-backed unsigned endpoints remain deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| Integer type aliases | 🟡 | medium | MySQL creation-time rewrites for `INT1`, `INT2`, `INT3`, `INT4`, `INT8`, and `MIDDLEINT`, including DESCRIBE and SHOW CREATE metadata. | Internal descriptor and parser normalize `INT1`, `INT2`, `INT3`, `INT4`, `INT8`, and `MIDDLEINT` to MySQL 8.4.9 canonical integer families. `INT0`, `INT5`, `INT6`, `INT7`, and `INT9` remain rejected. DESCRIBE and SHOW CREATE output wait for executable table DDL. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md). |
| Integer signedness attributes | 🟡 | top | `SIGNED` and `UNSIGNED` attribute syntax, range selection, metadata, and value enforcement. | Parser accepts repeated `SIGNED`/`UNSIGNED` on integer types and the descriptor treats any `UNSIGNED` as the effective unsigned type, matching MySQL 8.4.9 metadata. Executable DML now enforces signed/unsigned `TINYINT`, `SMALLINT`, `MEDIUMINT`, `INT`, and covered `BIGINT` endpoints for write paths, including exact `BIGINT UNSIGNED` assignment and `AUTO_INCREMENT` values above signed 64-bit range. Broad unsigned arithmetic remains deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| Integer display width compatibility | 🟡 | medium | Deprecated integer display width syntax, range checking, metadata rewrites, and SHOW CREATE behavior. | Parser accepts integer display-width syntax and rejects syntactically malformed negative widths; descriptor accepts widths `0..255`, rejects widths above `255`, and preserves MySQL 8.4.9 `tinyint(1)` metadata behavior. `ZEROFILL` and SHOW CREATE output are deferred. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md). |
| `DECIMAL` / `NUMERIC` | 🟡 | top | Exact precision math, storage limits, rounding, overflow, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes default and declared precision/scale, aliases, `SIGNED`/`UNSIGNED`, `ZEROFILL`, `DATA_TYPE`, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; parse-only column declarations are accepted. Exact storage, rounding, warnings, table DDL execution, and catalog writes are deferred. See [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md). |
| `FIXED` | 🟡 | medium | Alias behavior for `DECIMAL`, including creation-time metadata rewrites. | Internal descriptor and parser normalize `FIXED` to decimal metadata for parse-only declarations. Other-vendor compatibility type. See [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md). |
| `FLOAT` | 🟡 | high | Approximate numeric parsing, rounding, special cases, and metadata. | Internal descriptor supports default `FLOAT`, binary precision selector `FLOAT(p)`, display/scale `FLOAT(M,D)`, `SIGNED`/`UNSIGNED`, `ZEROFILL`, and MySQL 8.4.9 metadata; parse-only column declarations are accepted. Executable DML now applies single-precision target rounding and strict/non-strict/`INSERT IGNORE` finite-endpoint clipping for covered write paths. Exhaustive display formatting, warning ordering for mixed truncation/range cases, table DDL metadata fidelity, and catalog edge cases remain deferred. See [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md) and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `DOUBLE` / `REAL` | 🟡 | high | Approximate numeric parsing, rounding, REAL_AS_FLOAT mode, and metadata. | Internal descriptor supports `DOUBLE`, `DOUBLE PRECISION`, default-mode `REAL`, display/scale `DOUBLE(M,D)` and `REAL(M,D)`, numeric attributes, and MySQL 8.4.9 metadata; parse-only column declarations are accepted. Expression-level `CAST`/`CONVERT` honors `REAL_AS_FLOAT` for `REAL` targets, and executable DML now clips out-of-range `DOUBLE` assignments to finite endpoints in non-strict and `INSERT IGNORE` paths while strict mode rejects them with 1264. Exhaustive display formatting, warning ordering for mixed truncation/range cases, table DDL metadata fidelity, catalog edge cases, and broader mode-sensitive DDL behavior remain deferred. See [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md), [CAST expression spec](docs/specs/cast-expression/specs.md), and [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `FLOAT4` / `FLOAT8` | 🟡 | medium | Alias behavior for `FLOAT` and `DOUBLE`, including creation-time metadata rewrites. | Internal descriptor and parser normalize `FLOAT4` to `float` and `FLOAT8` to `double` metadata for parse-only declarations. Other-vendor compatibility types. See [numeric column types spec](docs/specs/numeric-column-types/specs.md). |
| `BIT` | 🟡 | high | Bit-value storage, literals, display, and numeric/string conversion. | `CREATE TABLE` supports `BIT` and `BIT(1..64)` with MySQL-compatible `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS`, `SHOW COLUMNS`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, table-backed result type metadata, and bit/hex literal inserts into `BIT` columns with fixed-width byte storage plus strict/`IGNORE` overflow diagnostics. Broader integer/string expression coercion, `HEX(BIT)` display semantics, default-value evaluation, and ALTER conversion paths remain deferred. See [BIT column type spec](docs/specs/bit-column-type/specs.md) and [hex and bit literals spec](docs/specs/hex-bit-literals/specs.md). |
| `BOOL` / `BOOLEAN` | 🟡 | top | Alias behavior for TINYINT(1) and expression truth rules. | Parser and descriptor implement column-type alias normalization to signed `tinyint(1)` metadata and reject width/signedness clauses on the aliases. Expression truth rules are deferred to expression semantics work. See [integer and boolean column types spec](docs/specs/integer-boolean-column-types/specs.md). |
| `SERIAL` | 🟡 | medium | Alias expansion to BIGINT UNSIGNED NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT UNIQUE. | `CREATE TABLE` supports `SERIAL` as a column type and `SERIAL DEFAULT VALUE` as an integer-column attribute, including normalized `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS`, `STATISTICS`, `SHOW COLUMNS`, `SHOW CREATE TABLE`, and omitted-value auto-increment generation for supported base tables. Nullable auto-increment edge cases, ALTER redefinition paths, and broader auto-increment diagnostics remain deferred. See [SERIAL column aliases spec](docs/specs/serial-column-alias/specs.md). |
| `DATE` | 🟡 | high | Date range, zero dates, invalid dates, literals, casts, and formatting. | Internal descriptor normalizes parse-only `DATE` declarations to MySQL 8.4.9 `DATA_TYPE`, `COLUMN_TYPE`, storage, range, and null `DATETIME_PRECISION` metadata. Table DDL execution, catalog writes, value storage, zero-date behavior, SQL modes, literals, casts, and formatting are deferred. See [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md). |
| `TIME` | 🟡 | high | Time range, fractional seconds, negative values, casts, and formatting. | Parser and descriptor accept `TIME` and `TIME(fsp)` for `fsp` 0..6, normalize MySQL-compatible `COLUMN_TYPE` and `DATETIME_PRECISION`, and reject overflow precision tokens without wrapping. Table DDL execution, storage, rounding/truncation, warnings, casts, and formatting are deferred. See [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md). |
| `DATETIME` | 🟡 | top | Datetime range, fractional seconds, defaults, casts, and formatting. | Parser and descriptor accept `DATETIME` and `DATETIME(fsp)` for `fsp` 0..6 and normalize parse-only metadata. Defaults, `ON UPDATE`, zero values, SQL modes, table DDL execution, catalog writes, storage, casts, and formatting are deferred. See [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md). |
| `TIMESTAMP` | 🟡 | high | UTC conversion, range, fractional seconds, defaults, and ON UPDATE. | Parser and descriptor accept `TIMESTAMP` and `TIMESTAMP(fsp)` for `fsp` 0..6 and normalize parse-only metadata. Default/nullability behavior, `ON UPDATE`, `explicit_defaults_for_timestamp`, time zone conversion, zero values, storage, table DDL execution, and catalog writes are deferred. See [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md). |
| `YEAR` | 🟡 | medium | Year storage, two-digit handling, casts, and display. | Parser and descriptor accept `YEAR` and deprecated column-type `YEAR(4)`, normalize both to `year` metadata, and reject other widths including overflow integer tokens without wrapping. Expression-level `CAST(... AS YEAR)` and `CONVERT(..., YEAR)` are implemented with MySQL 8.4.9-compatible two-digit pivots, string-zero handling, range diagnostics, warning codes, and `YEAR` result metadata. Deprecation warning 1287, table DDL input conversion, catalog writes, storage, and display semantics are deferred. See [temporal column types spec](docs/specs/temporal-column-types/specs.md), [CAST expression spec](docs/specs/cast-expression/specs.md), and [CONVERT cast expressions spec](docs/specs/convert-function/specs.md). |
| `CHAR` | 🟡 | high | Fixed-length string semantics, padding, charsets, collations, and metadata. | Internal descriptor normalizes length, charset/collation, binary-character-set rewrites, `BINARY` and `BYTE` attributes, and MySQL 8.4.9 `DATA_TYPE`/`COLUMN_TYPE`; parse-only column declarations are accepted. Value storage, padding, warnings, catalog writes, and executable DDL are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `VARCHAR` | 🟡 | top | Variable string semantics, length limits, charsets, collations, and metadata. | Internal descriptor validates required length, charset-sensitive limits, charset/collation metadata, binary-character-set rewrites, and `BINARY`/`BYTE` attributes; parse-only column declarations are accepted. Row-size diagnostics, value storage, warnings, catalog writes, and executable DDL are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `CHARACTER` / `CHARACTER VARYING` | 🟡 | medium | Alias behavior for `CHAR` and `VARCHAR`, including creation-time metadata rewrites. | Parser and descriptor normalize aliases to MySQL-compatible `char` and `varchar` metadata for parse-only declarations. SHOW CREATE output waits for executable table DDL. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `NCHAR` / `NATIONAL CHAR` | 🟡 | medium | Standard national-character aliases using MySQL's predefined `utf8mb3` character set. | Parser and descriptor normalize national character declarations to `char` with `utf8mb3` metadata. Deprecation warnings and SHOW CREATE output are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `NVARCHAR` / `NATIONAL VARCHAR` | 🟡 | medium | Standard national-character aliases using MySQL's predefined `utf8mb3` character set. | Parser and descriptor normalize national varchar declarations to `varchar` with `utf8mb3` metadata. MySQL warning 3720 and SHOW CREATE output are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `BINARY` | 🟡 | high | Fixed-length binary semantics and padding. | Internal descriptor normalizes length, binary metadata, and MySQL 8.4.9 `COLUMN_TYPE`; parse-only declarations are accepted. Padding/value storage and executable DDL are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `VARBINARY` | 🟡 | high | Variable binary semantics and length limits. | Internal descriptor validates required length and binary metadata; parse-only declarations are accepted. Row-size diagnostics, value storage, and executable DDL are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `CHAR BYTE` | 🟡 | low | Alias behavior for `BINARY`, including metadata rewrites. | Parser and descriptor normalize `CHAR BYTE`, `CHAR(n) BYTE`, and `VARCHAR(n) BYTE` to binary/varbinary metadata for parse-only declarations. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md). |
| `TINYBLOB` | 🟡 | medium | Binary large object length, metadata, and comparison semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity and null charset/collation metadata; supported executable table DDL now enforces the 255-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` paths. Broader storage/comparison semantics and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `BLOB` | 🟡 | high | Binary large object length, metadata, and comparison semantics. | Internal descriptor maps optional `BLOB(M)` lengths to MySQL-compatible blob families and metadata; supported executable table DDL now enforces the plain `BLOB` 65,535-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` paths. Broader storage/comparison semantics and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `MEDIUMBLOB` | 🟡 | medium | Binary large object length, metadata, and comparison semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity and null charset/collation metadata; supported executable table DDL now enforces the 16,777,215-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `INSERT ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, and single-table `UPDATE` / `UPDATE IGNORE` paths. Storage/comparison semantics and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `LONGBLOB` | 🟡 | medium | Binary large object length, metadata, and comparison semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity and null charset/collation metadata; supported executable table DDL now routes covered small-value writes through binary DML coercion so approximate numeric values store as MySQL-shaped text bytes and invalid UTF-8 source bytes are preserved. Maximum-size exhaustion, broader storage/comparison semantics, and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `TINYTEXT` | 🟡 | top | Text length, charset/collation, metadata, and index-prefix semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity, charset/collation metadata, and binary-character-set normalization; supported executable table DDL now enforces the 255-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` paths, including UTF-8 boundary truncation for the verified non-strict slice and conflict-row preservation for strict `REPLACE` failures. Broader storage/comparison/index-prefix semantics, charset validation, and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `TEXT` | 🟡 | top | Text length, charset/collation, metadata, and index-prefix semantics. | Internal descriptor maps optional `TEXT(M)` lengths by effective charset byte capacity, validates charset/collation clauses, and normalizes `CHARACTER SET binary` to blob metadata; supported executable table DDL now enforces the plain `TEXT` 65,535-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, single-table `UPDATE`, and single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` paths, including UTF-8 boundary truncation for the verified non-strict slice and conflict-row preservation for strict `REPLACE` failures. Broader storage/comparison/index-prefix semantics, charset validation, and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `MEDIUMTEXT` | 🟡 | top | Text length, charset/collation, metadata, and index-prefix semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity and charset/collation metadata; supported executable table DDL now enforces the 16,777,215-byte write limit for covered `INSERT ... VALUES`, `INSERT ... SET`, `INSERT IGNORE`, `REPLACE ... VALUES`, `REPLACE ... SET`, and single-table `UPDATE` / `UPDATE IGNORE` paths, including UTF-8 boundary truncation for the verified non-strict slice and conflict-row preservation for strict `REPLACE` failures. Storage/comparison/index-prefix semantics and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `LONGTEXT` | 🟡 | top | Text length, charset/collation, metadata, and index-prefix semantics. | Internal descriptor records MySQL-compatible capacity and charset/collation metadata; supported executable table DDL now routes covered small-value writes through text DML coercion so approximate numeric values store as MySQL-shaped text and invalid `utf8mb4` bytes reject or demote with valid-prefix storage like MySQL. Maximum-size exhaustion, broader storage/comparison/index-prefix semantics, and remaining write forms are deferred. See [string and binary column types spec](docs/specs/string-binary-column-types/specs.md) and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `LONG` / `LONG VARCHAR` | 🟡 | medium | Alias behavior for `MEDIUMTEXT`, including creation-time metadata rewrites. | Parser accepts `LONG VARCHAR` as parse-only `mediumtext`; storage and SHOW CREATE output are deferred. Full MySQL numeric coercion outside the covered cast and DML paths, signedness, overflow, and broader protocol metadata remain deferred. See [hex and bit literals spec](docs/specs/hex-bit-literals/specs.md), [CAST expression spec](docs/specs/cast-expression/specs.md), and [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| String literals | 🟡 | top | Escapes, introducers, national character set, binary literals, sql_mode effects, and concatenation. | Single- and double-quoted literals are parsed in the current lexical SQL mode, default-mode backslash escapes and `NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES` literal decoding are implemented for scalar expressions, supported DML storage, `LIKE`, `SHOW ... LIKE`, and SQL-level prepared-statement mode capture. Hex and bit literal byte evaluation is implemented separately. Character-set introducers, national string conversion, adjacent literal concatenation, and full collation-sensitive semantics remain deferred. See [NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/no-backslash-escapes/specs.md), [ANSI_QUOTES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/ansi-quotes-sql-mode/specs.md), and [hex and bit literals spec](docs/specs/hex-bit-literals/specs.md). |
| Temporal literals | 🟡 | top | DATE/TIME/TIMESTAMP literal syntax and coercion. | First slice implements `DATE`, `TIME`, and `TIMESTAMP` string literal expression syntax in supported scalar and DML source paths, including adjacent keyword/string tokens, default-mode double-quoted string tokens, `ANSI_QUOTES` rejection of double-quoted literal text, MySQL-compatible unaliased labels, typed temporal text evaluation, and observed MySQL 8.4.9 result metadata for DATE, TIME, and TIMESTAMP literal expressions. Exhaustive value validation and error 1525 diagnostics, fractional rounding beyond six digits, ODBC temporal escapes, and broad temporal conversion/comparison/arithmetic behavior remain deferred. See [temporal literals spec](docs/specs/temporal-literals/specs.md). |
| JSON path literals | 🟡 | high | Path grammar, quoting, wildcards, ranges, and errors. | Implemented for the JSON path extraction slice, including root, member names, quoted member names, array indexes, `last`, `last - N`, ranges, member and array wildcards, recursive descent, multi-match autowrapping, MySQL-compatible missing-path SQL `NULL` behavior, and diagnostics for invalid paths and paths disallowed by `JSON_KEYS()`. Exhaustive charset, SQL-mode, and native binary JSON-column path interactions remain deferred. See [JSON path extraction spec](docs/specs/json-path-extraction/specs.md). |
| User variables | 🟡 | high | Type retention, coercion, assignment, charset/collation, and result metadata. | First slice implemented for per-connection `@var` storage, unquoted and quoted names, case-insensitive lookup, `SET @var = expr` / `:=`, statement-start evaluation for multiple assignments, `@var := expr` expression assignment in scalar SELECT, table SELECT projection, and SET RHS expressions with immediate side effects and warning 1287, expression reads in scalar SELECT, table SELECT, supported DML predicates/assignments, unset-variable `NULL` reads, and MySQL-compatible result metadata for unset, assigned `NULL`, default `utf8mb4` text, integer, unsigned, approximate values, and covered assignment expressions in the covered expression paths. Full type coercion history, non-default character-set edge cases, broad assignment-expression support in every DML/grouped-query context, Performance Schema visibility, binary protocol interactions, and stored-program local variables remain deferred. See [user-variable foundation spec](docs/specs/user-variable-foundation/specs.md). |
| Local variables | ❌ | medium | Stored-program variable typing, scope, and diagnostics. |  |
| Type conversion | ❌ | top | Expression, comparison, assignment, insert/update, aggregate, and function argument conversion rules. | Task 16 specifies the first reusable expression conversion subset for scalar operators, including NULL propagation, truthiness, numeric/string comparison conversion warnings, division-by-zero warnings, unsigned bitwise behavior, and embedded-NUL text numeric conversions for covered scalar paths. DML assignment conversion now covers integer/decimal numeric coercion, signed/unsigned `TINYINT`/`SMALLINT`/`MEDIUMINT`/`INT` range clipping, covered signed/unsigned `BIGINT` range clipping and exact `BIGINT UNSIGNED` endpoint storage, covered `BIGINT UNSIGNED AUTO_INCREMENT` generation above signed 64-bit range, first-slice `FLOAT`/`DOUBLE` target range clipping, embedded-NUL text handling for covered integer and approximate numeric writes, MySQL-shaped 1366 source-text diagnostics for covered incorrect integer/decimal writes, selected-row diagnostics for direct table-backed `INSERT ... SELECT` coercions, temporal coercion, non-strict `NOT NULL` coercion, `CHAR`/`VARCHAR` length coercion for covered write paths, fixed `BINARY(N)` right-padding and `BINARY`/`VARBINARY` byte-length coercion for covered insert/update paths, `TINYTEXT`/`TINYBLOB` 255-byte write limits, plain `TEXT`/`BLOB` 65,535-byte write limits for covered insert, replace, and update paths, first-slice `MEDIUMTEXT`/`MEDIUMBLOB` 16,777,215-byte write limits for covered insert, ODKU, replace, and update paths, first-slice `LONGTEXT`/`LONGBLOB` small-value string/binary coercion including approximate numeric storage text and invalid UTF-8 handling for covered insert paths, and four-byte UTF-8 rejection/demotion for `utf8mb3` `VARCHAR`/`TEXT` insert and single-table update paths. Full aggregate, function, temporal, JSON, unsigned arithmetic beyond signed 64-bit range, exhaustive floating-point display/warning-order behavior, long TEXT/BLOB maximum-size exhaustion, remaining medium TEXT/BLOB write forms, and collation coercion rules remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md), [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md), [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md), and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| Collation coercibility | ❌ | top | Coercibility ranks, illegal mix diagnostics, and result collation derivation. |  |

### 4.2 Collations and comparison behavior

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Collation catalog entries | 🟡 | top | Expose every MySQL 8.4 collation through metadata, SHOW COLLATION, and charset/collation validation. | Internal registry validates and exposes `binary`, `latin1_swedish_ci`, `latin1_bin`, `utf8mb3_general_ci`, `utf8mb3_bin`, `utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci`, `utf8mb4_bin`, `utf8mb4_general_ci`, `utf8mb4_unicode_520_ci`, and `utf8mb4_unicode_ci` through `SHOW COLLATION` and selectable `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATIONS` / `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATION_CHARACTER_SET_APPLICABILITY` system views; all remaining MySQL collations and full collation semantics are deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md), [SHOW COLLATION spec](docs/specs/show-collation/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATIONS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-collations/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATION_CHARACTER_SET_APPLICABILITY spec](docs/specs/information-schema-collation-character-set-applicability/specs.md), [utf8mb4 unicode 520 collation spec](docs/specs/utf8mb4-unicode-520-ci/specs.md), and [utf8mb4 unicode collation spec](docs/specs/utf8mb4-unicode-ci/specs.md). |
| Default collation selection | 🟡 | top | Match server, database, table, column, literal, and expression default collation resolution. | Implemented for server defaults, schema defaults, `SET NAMES`, `SET CHARACTER SET`, and schema DDL over the initial registry; table, column, literal, and expression default resolution are deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md). |
| Unicode Collation Algorithm families | 🟡 | top | Match utf8mb4_0900, unicode, language-specific, accent-sensitive, case-sensitive, and kana-sensitive behavior where MySQL exposes it. | `utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci`, `utf8mb4_unicode_520_ci`, and `utf8mb4_unicode_ci` are recognized and exposed with MySQL-compatible catalog metadata for supported DDL/session validation. Full Unicode weight tables, contractions, expansions, accent/case/kana sensitivity, and optimizer/index interactions remain deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md), [utf8mb4 unicode 520 collation spec](docs/specs/utf8mb4-unicode-520-ci/specs.md), and [utf8mb4 unicode collation spec](docs/specs/utf8mb4-unicode-ci/specs.md). |
| Binary collations | 🟡 | top | Match binary string ordering, equality, case sensitivity, and metadata flags. | `binary`, `latin1_bin`, `utf8mb3_bin`, and `utf8mb4_bin` are recognized for validation and session/schema defaults; comparison, ordering, and metadata flags are deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md). |
| PAD SPACE and NO PAD collations | ❌ | top | Match trailing-space comparison, uniqueness, ordering, and index behavior. |  |
| Collation coercibility rules | ❌ | top | Match coercibility ranks, illegal mix diagnostics, and result collation derivation. |  |
| WEIGHT_STRING behavior | ❌ | medium | Match sort weight generation for supported collations and strings. |  |

## 5. Built-in functions and operators

This table is generated from the MySQL 8.4 built-in function and operator reference. Internal-only entries are listed because applications and metadata queries can still observe their names or diagnostics.

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `&` | 🟡 | top | Bitwise AND | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `<=>` | 🟡 | top | NULL-safe equal to operator | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases. Task 29 also implements uncorrelated multi-element row scalar subquery comparison with null-safe row-subquery truth semantics and `NOT_NULL` metadata in no-table scalar `SELECT` and current table-backed `SELECT` projection, `WHERE`, join `ON`, `HAVING`, and `ORDER BY` contexts. MyLite rejects `<=> ANY` syntax in the quantified subquery grammar. Broader row expression contexts, full SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, correlation, DML contexts, and general row quantified comparisons remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md) and [row subquery predicates spec](docs/specs/row-subquery-predicates/specs.md). |
| `%, MOD` | 🟡 | top | Modulo operator | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases, including covered unsigned `BIGINT` range behavior above signed 64-bit values. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. | `COUNT()` | 🟡 | top | Return a count of the number of rows returned | Implemented for the aggregate slice, including `COUNT(*)`, `COUNT(expr)`, implicit and grouped aggregates over single-table and joined row sources, empty-input zero, no-table top-level aggregate calls, metadata, `HAVING`, `ORDER BY`, and diagnostics for invalid placement/arity. Window execution, subqueries as row sources, and optimizer pushdown remain deferred. Window execution, joins beyond current aggregate support, subqueries, full charset/collation fidelity, spill-to-storage, and other aggregate-local `DISTINCT` families remain deferred. See [COUNT DISTINCT aggregate spec](docs/specs/count-distinct-aggregate/specs.md) and [aggregate functions and grouping spec](docs/specs/aggregate-grouping/specs.md). |

Exact native arity diagnostics and full binary-string type propagation remain deferred. | `STR_TO_DATE()` | 🟡 | high | Convert a string to a date | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including date, time, datetime, fractional-second, month-name, day-of-year, ordinal-day, meridiem, dynamic-format, trailing-garbage warning, invalid-value warning, NULL, metadata, charset/collation introspection, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, and strict DML warning promotion behavior. SQL-mode-specific zero and incomplete date materialization, week-year reconstruction, locale catalogs beyond English names, exact native arity diagnostics, and prepared-statement parameter metadata refinements remain deferred. See [STR_TO_DATE function spec](docs/specs/str-to-date-function/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `STRCMP()` | 🟡 | high | Compare two strings | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including exact two-argument acceptance, `NULL` short-circuiting without evaluating later warning-producing expressions, numeric-to-string conversion without truncation warnings, signed `LONGLONG` metadata with display length `2`, argument-nullability-aware `NOT_NULL` flags, projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and deterministic PAD SPACE / NO PAD behavior for collations in MyLite's current charset registry. Full Unicode collation weights, explicit character-set introducers, mixed-collation diagnostics, `BINARY expr` warning behavior, and exact native 1582 arity error-code exposure remain deferred. See [STRCMP function spec](docs/specs/strcmp-function/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUBDATE()` | 🟡 | top | Synonym for DATE_SUB() when invoked with three arguments | Implemented for the `SUBDATE(expr, INTERVAL expr unit)` form over the current scalar expression call sites, sharing the first date-arithmetic slice with `DATE_SUB()`. Supported units are `DAY`, `WEEK`, `MONTH`, `YEAR`, `HOUR`, `MINUTE`, and `SECOND`, including `NULL` propagation, invalid temporal warnings, integer interval conversion warnings, month-end clipping, negative intervals, DML warning promotion, and metadata. The non-`INTERVAL` `SUBDATE(date, days)` overload, `MICROSECOND`, combined units, and general interval expressions remain deferred. See [DATE_ADD, DATE_SUB, ADDDATE, and SUBDATE interval functions spec](docs/specs/date-add-sub-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUBSTR()` | 🟡 | top | Return the substring as specified | Implemented as a `SUBSTRING()` synonym in the string-function slice, including comma and `FROM` / `FOR` forms, UTF-8 character offsets, `NULL` propagation, no-table results, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and covered metadata. Full charset/collation semantics, binary-string behavior, and exact native syntax diagnostics remain deferred. See [string function slice spec](docs/specs/string-functions-substring-trim/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUBSTRING()` | 🟡 | top | Return the substring as specified | Implemented in the string-function slice, including comma and `FROM` / `FOR` forms, positive/negative/zero positions, zero/negative lengths, UTF-8 character offsets, `NULL` propagation, no-table results, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and covered metadata. Full charset/collation semantics, binary-string behavior, and exact native syntax diagnostics remain deferred. See [string function slice spec](docs/specs/string-functions-substring-trim/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUBSTRING_INDEX()` | 🟡 | high | Return a substring from a string before the specified number of occurrences of the delimiter | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including positive, negative, zero, oversized, numeric, and string-converted counts; absent, empty, multi-character, case-sensitive, and UTF-8 delimiters; `NULL` propagation; conversion warnings; connection-charset metadata under `utf8mb4` and `latin1`; table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`; `UPDATE`; and `DELETE`. Broad collation coercion, binary-string-specific behavior, `INSERT` expression paths, and exact native arity diagnostics remain deferred. See [SUBSTRING_INDEX function spec](docs/specs/substring-index-function/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUBTIME()` | 🟡 | medium | Subtract times | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including time and datetime subtraction, typed `DATE`/`TIME`/`DATETIME` first-argument metadata, `NULL` short-circuiting, invalid interval handling, input/result clipping warnings, datetime overflow behavior, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `INSERT`/`REPLACE` source expressions, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and strict DML warning promotion. Exact native arity diagnostics, time-zone-sensitive `TIMESTAMP` behavior, broader temporal literal variants, dynamic-parameter metadata, and broader expression contexts remain deferred. See [ADDTIME and SUBTIME functions spec](docs/specs/addtime-subtime-functions/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `SUM()` | 🟡 | top | Return the sum | Implemented for the aggregate slice, including implicit and grouped aggregates over single-table and joined row sources, NULL skipping, empty-input `NULL`, numeric conversion warnings, no-table top-level aggregate calls, metadata, `HAVING`, and `ORDER BY`. `DISTINCT`, window execution, native fixed-point arithmetic, subqueries as row sources, and broad type/collation fidelity remain deferred. See [aggregate functions and grouping spec](docs/specs/aggregate-grouping/specs.md) and [joined GROUP BY spec](docs/specs/joined-group-by/specs.md). |

Full fixed-point DECIMAL runtime storage, exact native arity error-code exposure, and last-displayed-digit parity for every platform-libm tangent result remain deferred. See [basic trigonometric functions spec](docs/specs/trigonometric-basic-functions/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
