Binary-log/replication safety warnings, exact native arity diagnostics, and insert expression paths remain deferred. See [UUID function spec](docs/specs/uuid-function/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `UUID_SHORT()` | 🟡 | high | Return an integer-valued universal identifier | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including per-call increasing unsigned 64-bit values, handle-local generated server-byte surrogate/startup/counter state, MySQL-compatible unsigned `LONGLONG` metadata, binary numeric charset/collation introspection, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, and `DELETE`. Server system-variable integration, binary-log/replication safety warnings, exact native arity diagnostics, and insert expression paths remain deferred. See [UUID_SHORT function spec](docs/specs/uuid-short-function/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `UUID_TO_BIN()` | 🟡 | high | Convert string UUID to binary | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including canonical dashed, hyphenless, and braced canonical UUID forms, uppercase/lowercase hex, optional time-part swap flag, `NULL`, invalid UUID error 1411, 16-byte binary-string output, binary metadata, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, and `DELETE`. Exact native arity diagnostics and insert expression paths remain deferred. See [UUID validation and conversion functions spec](docs/specs/uuid-conversion-functions/specs.md) and [scalar built-in functions spec](docs/specs/scalar-built-in-functions/specs.md). |
| `VALIDATE_PASSWORD_STRENGTH()` | ❌ | low | Determine strength of password |  |
| `VALUES()` | 🟡 | top | Define the values to be used during an INSERT | Implemented for the scoped `INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE` surface as `VALUES(col)`, returning candidate-row values and recording MySQL 8.4 deprecation warning 1287 for each use. Outside ODKU, MySQL's deprecated `VALUES(col)` NULL-return behavior and warning 1681 plus exact parser restrictions for non-column arguments remain deferred; standalone `VALUES` query expressions are tracked by the statement-level `VALUES` row. See [INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE spec](docs/specs/insert-on-duplicate-key-update/specs.md). |
| `VAR_POP()` | ❌ | medium | Return the population standard variance |  |
| `VAR_SAMP()` | ❌ | medium | Return the sample variance |  |
| `VARIANCE()` | ❌ | medium | Return the population standard variance |  |
| `VERSION()` | 🟡 | top | Return a string that indicates the MySQL server version | Implemented as the MySQL compatibility target version (`8.4.9`) with MySQL-compatible scalar result metadata for the covered expression paths. The public C API `mylite_version()` remains the MyLite library version. Replication warnings and exact arity error code reporting remain deferred. See [session information functions spec](docs/specs/session-information-functions/specs.md). |
| `WAIT_FOR_EXECUTED_GTID_SET()` | ❌ | low | Wait until the given GTIDs have executed on the replica. |  |
| `WEEK()` | 🟡 | medium | Return the week number | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including one-argument default mode 0, all explicit modes, low-three-bit mode masking, `NULL` date and `NULL` mode behavior, invalid/incomplete temporal warning 1292, string/numeric mode conversion warnings, metadata, table projection/`WHERE`/`ORDER BY`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, and DML warning promotion. Mutable `default_week_format`, `WEEKOFYEAR()`, `YEARWEEK()`, `WEEKDAY()`, `EXTRACT(WEEK FROM ...)`, and exact native arity diagnostics remain deferred. See [WEEK function spec](docs/specs/week-function/specs.md). |
| `WEEKDAY()` | ❌ | medium | Return the weekday index |  |
| `WEEKOFYEAR()` | ❌ | medium | Return the calendar week of the date (1-53) |  |
| `WEIGHT_STRING()` | ❌ | medium | Return the weight string for a string |  |
| `XOR` | 🟡 | medium | Logical XOR | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `YEAR()` | 🟡 | high | Return the year | Implemented for supported scalar expression call sites, including MySQL `YEAR` result metadata, `NULL` propagation, MyLite's current date/datetime parser surface, incomplete-date year extraction, invalid temporal warning 1292, and DML warning promotion. Exact native warning-message variants and exact arity diagnostics remain deferred. See [temporal part scalar functions spec](docs/specs/temporal-part-functions/specs.md). |
| `YEARWEEK()` | ❌ | medium | Return the year and week |  |
| `\|` | 🟡 | medium | Bitwise OR | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `~` | 🟡 | medium | Bitwise inversion | Implemented for no-table scalar `SELECT` expressions through MyLite expression evaluation, with parser/AST support and runtime tests for Task 16 verified cases. Row expression contexts outside single-table SELECT `WHERE`, full metadata, SQL-mode variants, complete collation behavior, functions, casts, row constructors, and subqueries remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |

## 6. Metadata schemas

Metadata rows include base MySQL objects plus optional plugin, Enterprise, NDB Cluster, and debug/development objects documented or shipped with MySQL 8.4.9. Each implementation should match the target build's availability: present objects need result-shape and privilege tests, while absent optional objects need MySQL-compatible missing-object or unavailable-plugin diagnostics.

### 6.1 INFORMATION_SCHEMA tables

| Table | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ADMINISTRABLE_ROLE_AUTHORIZATIONS` | ❌ | low | Grantable users or roles for current user or role |  |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.APPLICABLE_ROLES` | ❌ | low | Applicable roles for current user |  |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHARACTER_SETS` | 🟡 | high | Available character sets | `SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHARACTER_SETS` exposes the shared supported MyLite charset registry with uppercase MySQL columns and numeric `MAXLEN`, case-insensitive quoted table resolution, and a `SYSTEM VIEW` row in `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES` / `SHOW TABLES FROM information_schema`. The table is also selectable through the shared information-schema system-view path for projections, filters, aliases, ordering, limits, duplicate modifiers, and aggregates where those SELECT features are implemented. MyLite exposes only implemented charsets; privilege filtering and exact MySQL field metadata remain deferred. See [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHARACTER_SETS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-character-sets/specs.md) and [composable information-schema SELECT spec](docs/specs/information-schema-composable-select/specs.md). |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS` | 🟡 | high | Table and column CHECK constraints | `SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS` exposes the exact four-column MySQL table shape as a read-only system view, resolves qualified names case-insensitively including quoted forms, returns rows for supported `CREATE TABLE` and `ALTER TABLE` CHECK metadata with MySQL-compatible generated names and clause text, and appears in `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES` / `SHOW TABLES FROM information_schema`. The table is also selectable through the shared information-schema system-view path for projections, filters, aliases, ordering, limits, duplicate modifiers, and aggregates where those SELECT features are implemented. Covered DML paths enforce enforced cataloged CHECK rows through the CHECK runtime, and direct result-column metadata is MySQL-shaped for all four columns. Privilege filtering remains deferred. See [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.CHECK_CONSTRAINTS spec](docs/specs/information-schema-check-constraints/specs.md), [CHECK constraints spec](docs/specs/check-constraints/specs.md), and [composable information-schema SELECT spec](docs/specs/information-schema-composable-select/specs.md). |
| `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATION_CHARACTER_SET_APPLICABILITY` | 🟡 | high | Character set applicable to each collation | `SELECT * FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLLATION_CHARACTER_SET_APPLICABILITY` exposes the shared supported MyLite collation registry as `COLLATION_NAME` / `CHARACTER_SET_NAME`, with case-insensitive quoted table resolution, MySQL-shaped result descriptors for both columns, and a `SYSTEM VIEW` row in `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TABLES` / `SHOW TABLES FROM information_schema`. ## 7. Runtime configuration, modes, and variables

### 7.1 Session and runtime state

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Default schema | 🟡 | top | DATABASE()/SCHEMA(), USE, schema-qualified names, and single-file mapping. | `USE` stores handle-owned default schema state, dropping the selected schema clears it, and `DATABASE()`/`SCHEMA()` return the selected schema or `NULL`. Broader schema-qualified object execution remains deferred. See [schema lifecycle spec](docs/specs/schema-lifecycle/specs.md) and [session information functions spec](docs/specs/session-information-functions/specs.md). |
| Connection character set state | 🟡 | top | character_set_client, character_set_connection, character_set_results, collation_connection, and SET NAMES behavior. | Handle-owned state is implemented for `SET NAMES`, `SET CHARACTER SET`, and focused direct session assignment for `character_set_client`, `character_set_results`, and `collation_connection` over the initial registry, with first-slice `SHOW VARIABLES` visibility and `@@` expression reads for session and default/global values; protocol metadata and broader direct system-variable assignment are deferred. See [character set/collation foundation spec](docs/specs/character-set-collation-foundation/specs.md), [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md), and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| Time zone state | 🟡 | top | global/session time_zone, system_time_zone, temporal functions, TIMESTAMP conversion, and named time zones. | `time_zone` is exposed through `SHOW VARIABLES`, `@@time_zone`, and session `SET time_zone` / `DEFAULT`; temporal scalar functions still use UTC as MyLite's fixed effective session time zone, and `system_time_zone` plus named-zone tables remain deferred. See [current temporal functions spec](docs/specs/current-temporal-functions/specs.md) and [WP priority server placeholders spec](docs/specs/wp-priority-server-placeholders/specs.md). |
| Autocommit state | 🟡 | top | autocommit behavior, implicit transactions, and transaction boundary metadata. | Default autocommit-on behavior is implemented for explicit Task 21 transaction statements: DML outside an explicit transaction commits statement-by-statement, DML inside an explicit transaction participates in the active transaction with statement-level savepoint rollback, and `AND NO CHAIN` resumes autocommit behavior. `SHOW VARIABLES` exposes `autocommit=ON`, and `@@autocommit` expression reads return `1`; direct `SET autocommit` remains deferred. See [transaction statements spec](docs/specs/transaction-statements/specs.md), [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md), and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| Last insert id | 🟡 | top | LAST_INSERT_ID(), OK packet insert id, explicit LAST_INSERT_ID(expr), and multi-row behavior. | `INSERT ... VALUES` and `INSERT ... SET` track generated `AUTO_INCREMENT` values and expose the session value through `mylite_last_insert_id()`, `@@last_insert_id`, and `SHOW VARIABLES`. SQL `LAST_INSERT_ID()` and `LAST_INSERT_ID(expr)` are implemented for supported scalar expression paths, including `NULL` reset and unsigned wrap for negative values. Protocol OK packet insert id and remaining insert-expression interactions remain deferred. See [INSERT ... VALUES spec](docs/specs/insert-values/specs.md), [INSERT ... SET spec](docs/specs/insert-set/specs.md), [session information functions spec](docs/specs/session-information-functions/specs.md), and [WP priority server placeholders spec](docs/specs/wp-priority-server-placeholders/specs.md). |
| Affected rows | 🟡 | top | CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS, changed rows vs matched rows, DDL/DML OK packets, and warning counts. | Statement affected rows are exposed through `mylite_affected_rows()` for SQLite-backed statements and custom statements, and SQL `ROW_COUNT()` reads the previous completed statement's value for supported DDL, DML, and result-set statements. MySQL protocol OK packets, `CLIENT_FOUND_ROWS`, broader matched-vs-changed update modes, and protocol warning counts remain deferred. See [INSERT ... VALUES spec](docs/specs/insert-values/specs.md), [INSERT ... SET spec](docs/specs/insert-set/specs.md), and [session information functions spec](docs/specs/session-information-functions/specs.md). |
| Warnings and diagnostics | 🟡 | high | SHOW WARNINGS, SHOW ERRORS, GET DIAGNOSTICS, warning count, sql_notes, and truncation warnings. | Public warning-count/code/message accessors expose current stored conditions, and SQL `SHOW WARNINGS` / `SHOW ERRORS` expose current handle-owned warning/error/note diagnostics with MySQL-compatible row/count result shapes, supported `LIMIT` forms, diagnostic preservation, and clearing on successful nondiagnostic statements. Existing scalar expression, `WHERE`, DDL, and DML warning paths feed the same diagnostics area; failed parse/prepare/execute paths now store or promote error conditions, and selected DDL compatibility paths store MySQL notes. `SHOW VARIABLES` exposes `warning_count=0`, `error_count=0`, `max_error_count=1024`, and `sql_notes=ON` for the statement itself, matching nondiagnostic clearing behavior, and `@@warning_count`, `@@error_count`, `@@max_error_count`, and `@@sql_notes` expression reads expose the current supported system-variable values. `GET DIAGNOSTICS`, broader notes, mutable `sql_notes`/`max_error_count`, full generated-condition counter semantics, protocol warning counts, complete SQLSTATE/error-code propagation, and broader statement warning integration remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md), [WHERE clause spec](docs/specs/where-clause/specs.md), [SHOW diagnostics spec](docs/specs/show-diagnostics/specs.md), [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md), and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| Prepared statement registry | 🟡 | top | Per-connection prepared statement namespace and deallocation behavior. | SQL-level per-connection registry is implemented for `PREPARE`, `EXECUTE`, `DEALLOCATE PREPARE`, and `DROP PREPARE`, including case-insensitive names, duplicate prepare replacing/removing the old entry before validating the new source, and close-time cleanup. Binary protocol statement IDs, `max_prepared_stmt_count`, Performance Schema visibility, and full metadata lifecycle behavior remain deferred. See [SQL prepared statements spec](docs/specs/sql-prepared-statements/specs.md). |
| Temporary table namespace | 🟡 | high | Per-session temporary table metadata and shadowing. | Implemented for supported `CREATE TEMPORARY TABLE` tables using connection-local catalog rows and SQLite TEMP physical tables, including idempotent temporary `IF NOT EXISTS`, temp-first executable table lookup, temp-shadowed `ALTER TABLE`, temp-shadowed table metadata statements, and independent temporary `AUTO_INCREMENT` state. Schema-wide metadata views remain persistent-only, matching MySQL's omission of temporary tables. Broader temporary-table DDL beyond the supported create/drop/alter and metadata paths remains deferred. See [temporary tables spec](docs/specs/temporary-tables/specs.md). |
| Role and privilege state | 🟡 | high | CURRENT_ROLE(), grants, roles, DEFINER/INVOKER, and privilege-check-visible metadata. | `CURRENT_ROLE()` is implemented as an embedded no-active-roles placeholder returning `NONE`, and `CURRENT_USER()` / bare `CURRENT_USER` are implemented as embedded session identity scalar functions. Role grants, `SET ROLE`, default roles, grant-table authentication, definers, invokers, and privilege filtering remain deferred. See [CURRENT_ROLE function spec](docs/specs/current-role-function/specs.md) and [session identity functions spec](docs/specs/session-identity-functions/specs.md). |
| Locks | ❌ | high | Named locks, table locks, metadata locks, backup locks, and lock diagnostics. |  |

### 7.2 SQL modes

| SQL mode | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `ALLOW_INVALID_DATES` | 🟡 | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognition now preserves calendar-invalid `DATE` and `DATETIME` values for covered DML write paths while still rejecting or coercing impossible shapes and `TIMESTAMP` invalid dates according to strictness; broader parser, expression, DDL, and full temporal conversion behavior remains deferred. See [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `ANSI` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `ANSI_QUOTES` | 🟡 | top | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognizes and exposes `ANSI_QUOTES`; ordinary statement preparation treats double-quoted text as identifiers when the mode is active and as string literals otherwise. SQL-level `PREPARE` captures this lexical mode and `EXECUTE` reuses it after later `sql_mode` changes. Exhaustive DDL/DML parser coverage, stored programs, binary protocol prepared statements, and broader mode interactions remain deferred. See [ANSI_QUOTES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/ansi-quotes-sql-mode/specs.md). |
| `ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO` | ❌ | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Task 16 specifies verified default-mode expression warnings for `/`, `DIV`, `%`, and `MOD`; complete SQL-mode interactions remain deferred. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `HIGH_NOT_PRECEDENCE` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Task 16 specifies default `!` and `NOT` precedence and intentionally defers this mode-sensitive parser behavior. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `IGNORE_SPACE` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO` | 🟡 | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognizes and exposes the mode, and supported `INSERT ... VALUES`, `INSERT ... SET`, and `REPLACE` paths store explicit numeric `0` in `AUTO_INCREMENT` columns instead of allocating a generated value. `NULL`, omitted values, and `DEFAULT` continue to allocate. Broader conversion warnings, unsupported row sources, and protocol insert-id details remain deferred. See [NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO SQL mode spec](docs/specs/no-auto-value-on-zero/specs.md). |
| `NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES` | 🟡 | top | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognizes and exposes `NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES`; ordinary statement preparation marks string literals parsed under the mode so scalar literal decoding, supported DML storage, `LIKE`, explicit `ESCAPE`, and `SHOW ... LIKE` use MySQL-compatible backslash behavior. Default-mode scalar `LIKE` matching is length-aware for decoded embedded NUL bytes. SQL-level `PREPARE` captures this lexical mode and `EXECUTE` reuses it after later `sql_mode` changes. Exhaustive DDL/DML parser coverage, character-set conversion, and binary protocol prepared statements remain deferred. See [NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/no-backslash-escapes/specs.md) and [ANSI_QUOTES SQL mode spec](docs/specs/ansi-quotes-sql-mode/specs.md). |
| `NO_DIR_IN_CREATE` | ❌ | low | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION` | ❌ | top | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `NO_UNSIGNED_SUBTRACTION` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `NO_ZERO_DATE` | 🟡 | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognition now controls invalid/zero `DATE`, `DATETIME`, and `TIMESTAMP` DML coercion for covered insert and update write paths: strict mode rejects with error 1292, while non-strict mode appends warning 1264 and coerces to the type zero value. Broader parser, expression, DDL, and full temporal conversion behavior remains deferred. See [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `NO_ZERO_IN_DATE` | 🟡 | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognition now controls invalid/zero-in-date `DATE`, `DATETIME`, and `TIMESTAMP` DML coercion for covered insert and update write paths: strict mode rejects with error 1292, while non-strict mode appends warning 1264 and coerces to the type zero value where MySQL does. Broader parser, expression, DDL, and full temporal conversion behavior remains deferred. See [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| `ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY` | 🟡 | top | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` storage, `SET [SESSION\|LOCAL] sql_mode` assignment, and `@@sql_mode` expression reads are implemented for recognized mode names, including the focused `REPLACE(@@SESSION.sql_mode, 'ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY', '')` removal idiom, and this mode controls MyLite's current grouped-query and `SELECT DISTINCT ... ORDER BY` strict validators. Primary-key and non-null unique-key functional-dependence inference is implemented for current single-table grouping. Broader functional-dependence inference and other mode-sensitive parser/expression/DDL/DML behavior remain deferred. See [SQL mode and ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY spec](docs/specs/sql-mode-only-full-group-by/specs.md) and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |
| `PAD_CHAR_TO_FULL_LENGTH` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `PIPES_AS_CONCAT` | ❌ | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Task 16 specifies default `||` logical-OR behavior and intentionally defers mode-sensitive string concatenation. See [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| `REAL_AS_FLOAT` | 🟡 | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. | Session `sql_mode` recognizes and exposes `REAL_AS_FLOAT`; expression-level `CAST(... AS REAL)` and `CONVERT(..., REAL)` now use MySQL 8.4.9-compatible `FLOAT` result metadata and single-precision rounded values when the mode is active. Column DDL/storage semantics and broader mode-sensitive behavior remain deferred. See [CAST expression spec](docs/specs/cast-expression/specs.md) and [CONVERT cast expressions spec](docs/specs/convert-function/specs.md). |
| `STRICT_ALL_TABLES` | ❌ | medium | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `STRICT_TRANS_TABLES` | ❌ | top | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `TIME_TRUNCATE_FRACTIONAL` | ❌ | low | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |
| `TRADITIONAL` | ❌ | high | Implement parser, expression, DDL, DML, conversion, warning, and error behavior affected by this mode. |  |

| `last_insert_id` | 🟡 | top | Expose MySQL-compatible value, scope, mutability metadata, SET behavior, and diagnostics. | Exposed as a session-only system variable through `SHOW VARIABLES` and `@@last_insert_id`, backed by MyLite's existing generated-id state. | `skip_external_locking` | 🟡 | medium | Expose MySQL-compatible value, scope, mutability metadata, SET behavior, and diagnostics. | Exposes MySQL 8.4.9's global read-only compatibility value through `SHOW VARIABLES`, `SHOW GLOBAL VARIABLES`, and `@@skip_external_locking` / `@@GLOBAL.skip_external_locking` as `ON` / `1`, while `SET skip_external_locking` and `SET @@GLOBAL.skip_external_locking` report MySQL-shaped read-only diagnostics. MyLite has no MySQL external-locking side effect. See [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md) and [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md). |

Binary-log side effects are not applicable to the embedded runtime. See [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md), [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md), and [WP priority server placeholders spec](docs/specs/wp-priority-server-placeholders/specs.md). |

Persisted global variables/startup options and complete mode-sensitive behavior outside these slices remain deferred. See [SHOW VARIABLES spec](docs/specs/show-variables/specs.md), [system variable expression reads spec](docs/specs/system-variable-expression-reads/specs.md), [SQL mode and ONLY_FULL_GROUP_BY spec](docs/specs/sql-mode-only-full-group-by/specs.md), and [NO_AUTO_VALUE_ON_ZERO SQL mode spec](docs/specs/no-auto-value-on-zero/specs.md). |

### 7.4 Server status variables

The exact value shape, counter lifetime, session/global visibility, optional plugin/build availability, and `SHOW STATUS`/`performance_schema` exposure must be verified per variable.

## 8. Wire protocol and client API surface

### 8.1 Connection, authentication, and packets

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Protocol 10 initial handshake | ❌ | top | Server greeting, connection id, auth plugin data, status flags, charset, and capability negotiation. |  |
| Capability flags | ❌ | top | Client/server capability negotiation and rejection semantics. |  |
| SSLRequest and TLS upgrade | ❌ | high | TLS negotiation, failure modes, and capability interactions. |  |
| Handshake Response 41 | ❌ | top | Username, auth response, database, auth plugin name, connection attributes, and charset. |  |
| `caching_sha2_password` | ❌ | high | Default MySQL 8 authentication plugin packet flow and RSA/TLS behavior. |  |
| `sha256_password` | ❌ | high | Password exchange packet flow and diagnostics. |  |
| `mysql_native_password` | ❌ | high | Deprecated plugin compatibility and MySQL 8.4 behavior. |  |
| Auth switch request/response | ❌ | high | Plugin switching packet flow. |  |
| Auth more data | ❌ | high | Multi-step authentication packet flow. |  |
| OK packet | ❌ | top | Affected rows, last insert id, status flags, warnings, session-state tracking, and EOF deprecation. |  |
| ERR packet | ❌ | top | Error code, SQLSTATE, message text, and fatal/nonfatal behavior. |  |
| EOF packet compatibility | ❌ | high | Legacy EOF packet behavior when CLIENT_DEPRECATE_EOF is not set. |  |
| Result set metadata | ❌ | top | Column count, column definitions, flags, charsets, decimals, schema/table/origin names, and EOF/OK termination. | Task 23 implements shared result-column descriptors and C API accessors for current scalar and one-table `SELECT` output, including labels, origin names, parenthesized and unary-positive base-column origin preservation, field type, flags, length, decimals, charset id, and nullability. SQL-level `EXECUTE` delegates result metadata after execution starts for the first prepared-statement slice. Information-schema system views now attach MySQL 8.4.9-derived descriptors for direct wildcard and catalog-backed selected paths over `SCHEMATA`, `TABLES`, `COLUMNS`, `STATISTICS`, `TABLE_CONSTRAINTS`, `KEY_COLUMN_USAGE`, `CHECK_CONSTRAINTS`, `REFERENTIAL_CONSTRAINTS`, and `COLLATION_CHARACTER_SET_APPLICABILITY`; `SHOW DATABASES`, `SHOW TABLES`, `SHOW TABLE STATUS`, static `SHOW VARIABLES`, `SHOW STATUS`, `SHOW CHARACTER SET`, `SHOW COLLATION`, diagnostic SHOW statements, table-maintenance statements, `SHOW COLUMNS` / `DESCRIBE`, `SHOW INDEX`, `SHOW CREATE DATABASE`, and `SHOW CREATE TABLE` now attach MySQL 8.4.9-derived descriptors for their SQLite-backed result sets. Conditional scalar functions `IF`, `IFNULL`, `NULLIF`, and `COALESCE`, numeric scalar functions `ABS`, `MOD`, `FLOOR`, `CEIL`, and `CEILING`, temporal formatting functions `DATE_FORMAT()` and `TIME_FORMAT()`, `RAND()` / `RAND(seed)`, and `FOUND_ROWS()` infer MySQL-shaped descriptors for the covered scalar domains. MySQL wire-protocol column-definition packet emission, optional metadata negotiation, EOF/OK termination behavior, binary-protocol prepared-statement metadata, and materialized `max_length` remain deferred. See [result metadata and expression labels spec](docs/specs/result-metadata-expression-labels/specs.md), [foreign key support spec](docs/specs/foreign-key-support/specs.md), and [SQL prepared statements spec](docs/specs/sql-prepared-statements/specs.md). |
| Text result rows | ❌ | top | Text protocol row encoding, NULLs, character sets, and length-encoded values. |  |
| Binary result rows | ❌ | high | Prepared statement row encoding, null bitmap, type encodings, and unsigned flags. |  |
| LOCAL INFILE request | ❌ | high | Client file upload request flow and security controls. |  |
| Compression protocol | ❌ | medium | Compressed packet framing and capability negotiation. |  |
| Zstandard compression | ❌ | medium | zstd compression negotiation and packet behavior. |  |
| Connection attributes | ❌ | high | Attribute parsing and exposure in performance_schema/session tables. |  |
| Session state tracking | ❌ | high | Schema, system variables, GTIDs, transaction state, and state-change notices. |  |

## 9. Error, warning, and result semantics

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Error code catalog | ❌ | top | Use MySQL 8.4 error numbers, SQLSTATE values, and message text where practical. |  |
| Warning code catalog | ❌ | top | Use MySQL 8.4 warning numbers, SQLSTATE values, message text, and ordering. |  |
| Diagnostics area | ❌ | top | Statement diagnostics, condition areas, row count, and GET DIAGNOSTICS integration. |  |
| Strict-mode errors | 🟡 | top | Escalation from warnings to errors under strict SQL modes. | Session `sql_mode` now gates required `NOT NULL` value handling, invalid/zero temporal value handling, integer/decimal/approximate numeric DML coercion, signed/unsigned `TINYINT`/`SMALLINT`/`MEDIUMINT`/`INT` range handling, first-slice `FLOAT`/`DOUBLE` target range handling, `CHAR`/`VARCHAR` length coercion for supported `INSERT ... VALUES`, `INSERT ... SET`, `REPLACE`, ODKU update assignments, single-table `UPDATE`, and joined `UPDATE`, `TINYTEXT`/`TINYBLOB` 255-byte length coercion, plain `TEXT`/`BLOB` 65,535-byte length coercion, first-slice `MEDIUMTEXT`/`MEDIUMBLOB` 16,777,215-byte length coercion, invalid UTF-8 byte rejection for covered `utf8mb4` `VARCHAR`/`TEXT` writes, and four-byte UTF-8 rejection for covered `utf8mb3` `VARCHAR`/`TEXT` writes: strict modes retain 1364/1048/1292/1264/1265/1366/1406 errors where MySQL does, including conflict-row preservation for strict `REPLACE` truncation failures, while non-strict mode applies MySQL-compatible implicit-default, temporal warning coercion, numeric warning/note coercion, integer and approximate range clipping, string truncation warning coercion, and valid-prefix UTF-8 storage for covered forms. `INSERT IGNORE`, single-table `UPDATE IGNORE`, and joined `UPDATE IGNORE` now demote covered `NOT NULL`, numeric/range, temporal, string-length, and invalid UTF-8 assignment/value errors to MySQL-compatible warnings and coerced values. Broader conversion, uncovered ranges, long TEXT/BLOB limits, remaining medium TEXT/BLOB write forms, remaining ODKU conversion demotion outside covered update assignments, `LOAD DATA`, and protocol warning/error behavior remain deferred. See [non-strict NOT NULL coercion spec](docs/specs/non-strict-not-null-coercion/specs.md), [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md), [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md), and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| IGNORE warning demotion | 🟡 | top | DML IGNORE conversion, duplicate, constraint, and truncation behavior. | Started for `INSERT IGNORE` duplicate, required-column, invalid integer text, signed/unsigned `TINYINT`/`SMALLINT`/`MEDIUMINT`/`INT` range clipping, first-slice `FLOAT`/`DOUBLE` target range clipping, invalid `DATE` text, `CHAR`/`VARCHAR` truncation demotion, invalid `utf8mb3` four-byte character demotion, `TINYTEXT`/`TINYBLOB` truncation demotion, and plain `TEXT`/`BLOB` plus first-slice `MEDIUMTEXT`/`MEDIUMBLOB` truncation demotion for covered value inserts; scoped ODKU update-branch duplicate and covered conversion demotion, including first-slice `MEDIUMTEXT`/`MEDIUMBLOB` update-assignment truncation; first-slice single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` duplicate-key plus child/parent foreign-key row skipping; single-table `UPDATE IGNORE` demotion for covered numeric, range, temporal, string-length, invalid `utf8mb3` four-byte character, and explicit `NULL` not-null assignment conditions including `TINYTEXT`/`TINYBLOB` 255-byte, plain `TEXT`/`BLOB` 65,535-byte, and first-slice `MEDIUMTEXT`/`MEDIUMBLOB` 16,777,215-byte assignments; and joined `UPDATE IGNORE` duplicate-key skipping plus covered assignment conversion demotion. Remaining insert broader conversion demotion and uncovered ranges, `DELETE IGNORE`, `LOAD DATA IGNORE`, and `CREATE TABLE ... SELECT IGNORE` behavior remain deferred. See [INSERT IGNORE spec](docs/specs/insert-ignore/specs.md), [UPDATE IGNORE spec](docs/specs/update-ignore/specs.md), [numeric DML coercion spec](docs/specs/numeric-dml-coercion/specs.md), and [string DML coercion spec](docs/specs/string-dml-coercion/specs.md). |
| Metadata flags | ❌ | top | Column flags for NOT NULL, PRI/UNI/MUL, BLOB, UNSIGNED, ZEROFILL, BINARY, ENUM, AUTO_INCREMENT, TIMESTAMP, SET, and NUM. |  |
| Result ordering guarantees | 🟡 | top | Match MySQL ordering only where SQL semantics or MySQL behavior require it. | Implemented for Task 18's `SELECT ... ORDER BY ... LIMIT/OFFSET` subset, supported joined row sources, the Task 25 aggregate grouping slice, and top-level query expressions over `UNION`, `INTERSECT`, `EXCEPT`, `TABLE`, and `VALUES`, with deterministic tests only when explicit sort keys fully define row order. Equal-key ordering remains intentionally unspecified, and optimizer-sensitive order preservation and full collation edge cases remain deferred. See [ORDER BY, LIMIT, and OFFSET spec](docs/specs/order-limit-offset/specs.md), [query expression forms spec](docs/specs/query-expression-forms/specs.md), [inner joins spec](docs/specs/inner-joins/specs.md), [outer joins spec](docs/specs/outer-joins/specs.md), and [aggregate functions and grouping spec](docs/specs/aggregate-grouping/specs.md). |
| Floating-point edge cases | ❌ | high | NaN/Inf handling, division, rounding, comparison, and platform-sensitive behavior. |  |
| Temporal edge cases | 🟡 | top | Zero dates, leap days, DST, fractional truncation/rounding, and timezone table behavior. | Invalid and zero `DATE`, `DATETIME`, and `TIMESTAMP` DML writes now follow MySQL 8.4.9-observed strict, non-strict, `NO_ZERO_DATE`, `NO_ZERO_IN_DATE`, and `ALLOW_INVALID_DATES` behavior for the currently supported insert and update paths. Typed `DATE`, `DATETIME`, and `TIMESTAMP` expression values compare valid ISO date/datetime strings in the temporal domain. Broader temporal input variants, invalid comparison diagnostics, scalar functions, timezone/DST behavior, fractional truncation and rounding, and metadata details remain deferred. See [temporal DML coercion spec](docs/specs/temporal-dml-coercion/specs.md) and [expression operator foundation spec](docs/specs/expression-operator-foundation/specs.md). |
| JSON edge cases | 🟡 | top | Duplicate keys, ordering, binary JSON storage-visible behavior, path errors, and partial update metadata. | Started for `JSON_OBJECT()` duplicate-key last-wins behavior and MySQL-observed key-order normalization in the scalar foundation. The path extraction slice covers invalid path diagnostics, missing-path SQL `NULL`, multi-match autowrapping, JSON `null` serialization, and `JSON_KEYS()` path-shape restrictions. Binary JSON storage-visible behavior, partial update metadata, comparison ordering, mutator semantics, and JSON column edge cases remain deferred. See [JSON scalar foundation spec](docs/specs/json-scalar-foundation/specs.md) and [JSON path extraction spec](docs/specs/json-path-extraction/specs.md). |
| Spatial edge cases | ❌ | medium | SRID mismatch, geographic vs Cartesian calculations, invalid geometry handling, and units. |  |
| Privilege-sensitive metadata | ❌ | high | Metadata visibility affected by grants and DEFINER/INVOKER context. |  |

## 10. Embedded-design compatibility decisions

Some MySQL server features do not naturally map to an in-process single-file database. They still belong in the compatibility matrix because applications may issue the syntax or inspect related metadata. Each row needs an explicit design decision before it can move out of `❌`.

| Feature | Status | Priority | Target behavior | Implementation notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Replication and binary logs | ❌ | low | Decide between unsupported errors, no-op placeholders, metadata stubs, or local change streams. |  |
| Account management and privileges | ❌ | high | Decide how much auth/privilege syntax is accepted and how metadata is represented without a server account model. |  |
| Resource groups | ❌ | low | Decide embedded diagnostics for CPU/thread scheduling features. |  |
| Components and plugins | ❌ | low | Decide loadable component/plugin syntax behavior and metadata exposure. |  |
| Server lifecycle commands | ❌ | low | Decide diagnostics for RESTART, SHUTDOWN, CLONE, and backup-lock operations. |  |
| Storage engines | 🟡 | high | Decide how ENGINE clauses, SHOW ENGINES, and engine-specific metadata map to SQLite-backed storage. | First storage-engine registry slice maps MyLite's SQLite-backed table storage to an `InnoDB` compatibility facade, keeps unsupported engine names rejected by `CREATE TABLE`, and exposes common unsupported MySQL engines through `SHOW ENGINES` and selectable `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ENGINES` rows. `SHOW ENGINE engine STATUS` adds a deterministic embedded InnoDB status row plus metadata-compatible empty rows for known empty-status engines. Full engine-specific table behavior, build/runtime-disabled states, full MySQL field metadata for information-schema SELECTs, and volatile engine internals are deferred. See [SHOW ENGINES spec](docs/specs/show-engines/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ENGINES spec](docs/specs/information-schema-engines/specs.md), [SHOW ENGINE STATUS spec](docs/specs/show-engine-status/specs.md), and [CREATE TABLE base execution spec](docs/specs/create-table-base-execution/specs.md). |
| `InnoDB` engine surface | 🟡 | high | Decide how MySQL's default engine metadata, syntax, and diagnostics map onto SQLite-backed storage. | First executable slices expose `InnoDB` as the default supported storage-engine facade in `SHOW ENGINES` and `INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ENGINES`, with transactions and savepoints supported and XA unsupported. `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` returns a deterministic embedded status row rather than MySQL's volatile InnoDB monitor text. It does not claim MySQL InnoDB internals, row locks, foreign-key enforcement, tablespaces, redo/undo logs, or XA. See [SHOW ENGINES spec](docs/specs/show-engines/specs.md), [INFORMATION_SCHEMA.ENGINES spec](docs/specs/information-schema-engines/specs.md), and [SHOW ENGINE STATUS spec](docs/specs/show-engine-status/specs.md). |
| `MyISAM` engine surface | ❌ | medium | Decide parser, metadata, SHOW ENGINES, and unsupported-feature diagnostics. |  |
| `MEMORY` engine surface | ❌ | high | Decide temporary/in-memory table compatibility and diagnostics. |  |
| `CSV` engine surface | ❌ | medium | Decide log-table and ENGINE=CSV compatibility behavior. |  |
| `ARCHIVE` engine surface | ❌ | low | Decide archive-engine syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `BLACKHOLE` engine surface | ❌ | low | Decide blackhole-engine syntax and embedded-compatible behavior. |  |
| `MERGE` engine surface | ❌ | low | Decide MERGE/MyISAM union table syntax and diagnostics. |  |
| `FEDERATED` engine surface | ❌ | low | Decide foreign-server table syntax, mysql.servers metadata, and diagnostics. |  |
| `NDB` engine surface | ❌ | low | Decide NDB-specific DDL syntax, metadata tables, and diagnostics. |  |
| `PERFORMANCE_SCHEMA` engine surface | ❌ | medium | Decide metadata and diagnostics for performance_schema engine tables. |  |
| Tablespaces and logfile groups | ❌ | low | Decide syntax acceptance and diagnostics for non-single-file storage constructs. |  |
| Performance Schema | ❌ | medium | Decide which tables expose real embedded runtime metrics versus documented empty placeholders. |  |
| sys schema | ❌ | medium | Decide which objects are useful over MyLite metadata and which should be documented as empty or unsupported. |  |
| File import/export | ❌ | high | Decide secure embedded behavior for LOAD DATA, LOAD XML, SELECT INTO OUTFILE, and SELECT INTO DUMPFILE. |  |
| User-defined/loadable functions | ❌ | medium | Decide C-extension registration model, security boundaries, and binary size policy. |  |
| X Protocol and Document Store | ❌ | low | Decide whether MySQL X Protocol and CRUD-style document APIs are out of scope, stubbed, or implemented. |  |

## 11. Test expectations

- Every implemented row needs MySQL 8.4.9 comparison tests.
- Tests must cover normal results, errors, warnings, metadata, affected rows, inserted ids, session state, and side effects.
- Syntax-only compatibility must still test parser acceptance and the exact warning/error/placeholder behavior.
- Feature work should add focused guide documentation when behavior is subtle enough that future maintainers need the design rationale.
- When MySQL behavior is platform-dependent or storage-engine-dependent, tests should document the chosen MyLite contract and the observed MySQL baseline.
